Motivation: 现有在线（流式）Transformer 3D重建方法在处理长图像流时忽略了两个关键信号：每个新到帧的重要性，以及模型内部状态的信息饱和程度。由此导致长序列中状态更新无差别累积、可塑性下降，性能显著退化。

Method: 提出Info3R信息自适应测试时训练方法：(1) 信息感知状态更新，依据新帧的冗余度与信息量调制状态更新强度；(2) 动态状态重置，以状态更新的累积幅度与模型预测置信度为触发条件，并配合anchor-to-world对齐，以恢复状态可塑性。

Result: 在相机位姿估计、视频深度估计与3D重建任务上取得一致性提升，显著缓解长序列评测中的性能退化；在KITTI Odometry上平均ATE较LongStream低1.68倍，展现对长时户外序列的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 将"帧信息量"与"状态饱和"显式建模为在线3D重建的状态管理信号，是提升流式重建长序列稳定性的有效方向。

Abstract: Transformer-based models have recently achieved strong performance on 3D reconstruction from images, and recent works extend them to process video streams in an online manner for real-world deployment. However, existing methods overlook two key signals when handling long image streams: the importance of each incoming frame and the information saturation of the model's internal state. In this paper, we propose Info3R, a novel information-adaptive test-time training method for the online 3D reconstruction. We introduce an information-aware state update that modulates the state update strength based on the redundancy and informativeness of each incoming frame. To restore the state's plasticity -- its capacity to incorporate new observations -- we propose a dynamic state reset, triggered by the cumulative magnitude of state updates and the model's prediction confidence and accompanied by an anchor-to-world alignment. Our method achieves consistent improvements on camera pose estimation, video depth estimation, and 3D reconstruction, while substantially mitigating the performance degradation in the long sequence evaluation. Notably, on KITTI Odometry, our method achieves on average 1.68x lower ATE than LongStream, demonstrating its robustness on extended outdoor sequences.

</details>


### [71] [PRIME: Perception Feedback with Situational Memory Embeddings in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22040)
*Erik Deinzer,Naya Baslan,Luca Paparusso,Narunas Vaskevicius,Peter Knott,Luigi Palmieri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRIME: 一种可学习的反馈机制，用"情境记忆"（Situational Memory）调制VLA的感知查询，实现意图驱动的感知注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动驾驶VLA模型主要依赖感知—推理—规划的前馈推理。虽然感知模块内部保留了时序循环，但早期感知对下游推理与导航目标"视而不见"，仅按视觉输入本身进行处理，无法根据既往决策优先关注关键线索，造成感知与决策目标脱节。

Method: 提出PRIME：维护一个Situational Memory，通过跨注意力聚合过去L步窗口内的感知、推理、导航目标与预测行为的隐表示；再以该记忆条件化VLA的感知查询，从而在感知阶段引入意图驱动的注意力。该方法只增加最多29.7M参数（7.3B基座模型的0.41%），计算开销极小。

Result: 在Bench2Drive闭环基准上，PRIME取得82.47的驾驶分数（比ORION高4.73）和60.00%的成功率（提升5.38个百分点），为已发表、在Think2Drive示教数据上训练的VLA中报告的最高驾驶分数。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在VLA层级中引入轻量的自顶向下反馈（记忆条件化感知）可显著提升闭环自动驾驶性能，且参数量代价极低，为感知—推理—规划的单向级联提供了廉价而有效的闭环补充。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for autonomous driving operate primarily through feedforward inference across the perception--reasoning--planning hierarchy. While modern architectures maintain temporal recurrence within the perceptual module, early perception remains blind to downstream reasoning and navigation goals, processing visual inputs agnostically without prioritizing cues informed by prior decisions. To bridge this gap, this paper introduces PRIME, a learned feedback mechanism that conditions the VLA perceptual queries on a novel Situational Memory. By aggregating latent representations of past perception, reasoning, navigation goals, and predicted behaviors across an L-step window via cross-attention, PRIME enables intent-driven perceptual attention at minimal computational cost, adding only a maximum of 29.7M parameters (0.41% of the 7.3B-parameter base model). Evaluated on the Bench2Drive closed-loop benchmark, PRIME achieves a state-of-the-art Driving Score of 82.47 (+4.73 over ORION) and a Success Rate of 60.00% (+5.38 percentage points), the highest reported Driving Score among published VLAs trained on Think2Drive demonstrations.

</details>


### [72] [Traffic Sign Recognition for Autonomous Driving Using Branched YOLOv2 and Geometric Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22060)
*Arefeh Rezaei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该工作基于YOLOv2构建交通标志检测与分类一体化系统：一是引入中间预测层形成分支结构以对简单样本提前退出、降低推理耗时；二是引入无监督贝叶斯分割生成的二值形状与类别几何模板进行比对，以减少相似标志的误分类，并以自建GTSDB+GTSRB融合数据集在10类标志上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通标志识别需同时高效完成定位与语义分类，是自动驾驶与ADAS的感知基础任务。基线YOLOv2在所有样本上计算开销固定，且对视觉上相近的标志易混淆，因此作者希望（1）通过分支式提前退出降低简单样本的计算量，（2）借助几何/形状信息纠正相似类之间的分类错误。

Method: 以YOLOv2为骨干同时做检测与分类。改进一：增加中间预测层形成分支架构（whole-image与cell-wise两种分支策略），可在简单样本上提前终止推理。改进二：用无监督贝叶斯图像分割产生二值表示，在YOLOv2边界框内与类别特定的几何模板比较，该几何信息可用于推理阶段或作为训练的额外监督信号。数据集由GTSDB与GTSRB经无缝克隆（seamless cloning）与受控图像变换合成，含10类标志、3000训练/300测试样本。

Result: 所选分支架构：运行时间0.647 s，mAP 0.680；基线YOLOv2为0.6607 s与0.680 mAP——分支带来少量速度提升但精度持平。推理阶段几何验证将mAP提升至0.713；几何特征训练变体达到0.697 mAP，运行时间0.6608 s。整体表明几何信息主要贡献于精度提升，而分支结构的速度收益有限。

Conclusion: 在YOLOv2上结合分支提前退出与几何形状校验可提升交通标志识别精度（mAP 0.680→0.713），但分支带来的推理加速相对于精度代价并不显著；几何信息在推理期使用的收益优于仅用于训练。

Abstract: Traffic sign recognition (TSR) is an important perception task for autonomous driving and advanced driver-assistance systems, where a system must both localize traffic signs and determine their semantic classes efficiently. This work presents a TSR system based on YOLOv2 for simultaneous detection and classification. Two complementary modifications are studied. First, YOLOv2 is extended with intermediate prediction layers, forming a branched architecture that can terminate inference early for easy cases and reduce computation time. Both whole-image and cell-wise branching strategies are investigated. Second, geometric information is introduced to reduce classification errors between visually similar signs. An unsupervised Bayesian image-segmentation method produces binary representations that are compared with class-specific geometric templates inside YOLOv2 bounding boxes. This information is used either during inference or as an additional signal during training. A dedicated dataset is constructed by combining GTSDB and GTSRB samples using seamless cloning and controlled image transformations. Experiments cover ten traffic-sign classes, with 3,000 training and 300 test samples. The selected branched architecture reports 0.647 s runtime and 0.680 mAP, compared with 0.6607 s and 0.680 mAP for baseline YOLOv2. Geometric verification during inference increases mAP to 0.713, while the geometric-feature training variant achieves 0.697 mAP with a reported runtime of 0.6608 s.

</details>


### [73] [OmniVBench: A Benchmark and Large-Scale Dataset for Omni Reference-to-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22069)
*Wenxue Li,Peiyan Guan,Haoyang Jiang,Junxian Cai,Hualuo Liu,Chunjie Zhang,Chong Guan,Songlian Li,Taiyi Wu,Yongjian Yu,Xiaotong Zhao,Alan Zhao,Eric Liu,Xi Chen,Yu Liu,Lei Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 OmniVBench 基准与 Omni-R2V 数据集，扩展参考视频生成（R2V）的参考类型、细粒度任务与多参考组合，并引入因子化清单评估；数据集含 34 万训练样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 R2V 基准测试用例参考类型与组合有限，评估仅关注整体一致性，无法判断参考因子是否被保真保留、解耦并正确路由到目标；同时 omni R2V 训练数据构建成本高、资源稀缺。

Method: 构建 OmniVBench：7 个任务族、18 个细粒度任务，覆盖内容、运动、风格、结构、叙事与多参考场景；设计因子落地评估，含 12,172 条案例级检查清单项，考察参考因子保留、解耦绑定与指令实现。基于专业视频语料构建 Omni-R2V Dataset，含 34 万处理后样本及任务专用参考-目标配对流程。

Result: 对先进开源与闭源 R2V 模型的评测显示，在不同任务族与评估维度上均存在明显性能差距，暴露当前模型的局限。

Conclusion: OmniVBench 与 Omni-R2V Dataset 为 omni R2V 提供评估与训练资源，但现有模型在多任务、多维度参考控制上仍有较大提升空间。

Abstract: Reference-to-video (R2V) generation is evolving toward increasingly general and versatile reference control, giving rise to the emerging paradigm of omni R2V generation. However, existing benchmarks fall short of these emerging capabilities: their test cases cover limited reference types and compositions, and their evaluation protocols largely assess holistic reference consistency, overlooking whether reference factors are properly preserved, disentangled, and routed. Meanwhile, the high cost of constructing omni R2V training data makes suitable training resources scarce. To address these gaps, we introduce OmniVBench and the Omni-R2V Dataset for evaluating and training omni R2V models. OmniVBench expands R2V evaluation across broader reference types, fine-grained control tasks, and richer reference compositions, covering 7 task families and 18 fine-grained tasks spanning content, motion, style, structure, narrative, and multi-reference settings. We introduce factor-grounded evaluation with 12,172 case-specific checklist items, assessing whether intended reference factors are faithfully preserved, correctly disentangled and bound to their targets, and properly realized according to the instruction. We further introduce the Omni-R2V Dataset, bringing industrial-grade training resources for diverse R2V tasks to the broader research community. Drawing primarily on a large-scale corpus of professional video footage, it comprises 340K processed training samples spanning diverse reference types and multi-reference compositions. We develop task-specific pipelines for reference-target pair construction, offering a practical and scalable recipe for omni R2V data construction. Extensive evaluation of advanced open- and closed-source R2V models reveals clear performance gaps across task families and evaluation dimensions on OmniVBench, highlighting remaining limitations of current R2V models.

</details>


### [74] [MintAct: A Unified Visual Agent for Digital Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22083)
*Mingfei Gao,Rui Tian,Haiming Gang,Bohan Zhai,Le Zhang,Yuanzheng Gong,Di Feng,Ege Özsoy,Kaixin Ma,Vishwesh Kirthivasan,Oğuzhan Fatih Kar,Roman Bachmann,Anders Boesen Lindbo Larsen,Afshin Dehghan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MintAct：统一的视觉-语言模型家族（2B/4B/8B），统一 UI grounding、跨移动/桌面/Web 多步导航和视觉工具使用，通过可扩展环境与异步 RL 基础设施，达到与各领域专家模型相当的性能，OSWorld-Verified 达 48.9。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 GUI/UI 智能体多依赖分领域专家模型，能力割裂，难以在不同平台（移动、桌面、Web）与任务（定位、导航、工具调用）间迁移；同时缺乏可扩展的环境与 RL 训练基础设施来支撑跨域大规模训练，尤其是噪声反馈与 off-policy 漂移下的稳定性问题。

Method: 构建异构 per-domain 后端、支持数百并发实例的环境基础设施，同时服务轨迹数据采集与在线 RL；采用异步 RL 框架，显式控制跨域训练分布，并在噪声环境反馈与 off-policy 漂移下保持训练稳定；在 2B/4B/8B 三个规模上统一训练 UI grounding、多步导航与视觉工具使用能力，并精细设计环境、数据与训练配方。

Result: MintAct 在同等模型规模下于多项基准上达到 SOTA，OSWorld-Verified 得分 48.9；统一模型在各类能力上匹配分领域专家模型的表现。

Conclusion: 通过环境、数据与训练配方的协同设计，单一家族模型可统一 UI 定位、跨平台导航与工具调用等 GUI 智能体能力，且可扩展的异步 RL 基础设施是实现该统一与性能达标的关键。

Abstract: We present MintAct, a family of vision-language models that unifies UI grounding, multi-step navigation across mobile, desktop, and web, and visual tool use, trained at 2B, 4B, and 8B scales. Through careful design of our environments, data, and training recipes, MintAct models match the performance of per-domain specialists across all of these capabilities. To enable this, we develop a scalable environment and reinforcement learning (RL) infrastructure. On the environment side, we host hundreds of concurrent instances across heterogeneous per-domain backends, serving both trajectory data collection and online RL. To enable efficient and scalable RL training, an asynchronous framework keeps explicit control over the cross-domain training distribution and remains stable under noisy environment feedback and off-policy drift. Experimental results show that MintAct achieves state-of-the-art performance (48.9 on OSWorld-Verified) across a wide range of benchmarks at comparable model sizes.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [75] [WM-VS: Progress-Aligned World Models for Closed-Loop Visual Servoing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20892)
*Guanzhong Sun,Junyi Ma,Yixuan Zhou,Yuxuan Wu,Yanzi Miao,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WM-VS：面向闭环视觉伺服的"目标中心-进度对齐"世界模型框架，用离线DINOv2目标区域对应定义4维伺服坐标，先对齐动作条件潜转移，再冻结世界模型训练反应式关节速度策略，实现仅RGB、无在线轨迹优化的闭环纠偏与零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环视觉伺服的核心需求是预测"动作是否能减小任务误差"，而现有世界模型只保证动作-结果预测"看起来合理"，二者之间存在prediction-control mismatch。作者将这一错配形式化，并主张以任务进度（误差收缩）而非动作合理性作为世界模型的训练信号。

Method: 离线用DINOv2在目标区域建立对应关系，导出带符号的4维伺服坐标（平移、尺度、面内旋转）。Stage 1：将动作条件潜转移与该伺服坐标对齐，使潜空间演化具备进度语义。Stage 2：冻结世界模型，训练反应式关节速度策略，监督包含动作模仿、后果监督以及偏好误差收缩的短时想象rollout。部署时为纯RGB反应式，无在线轨迹优化。

Result: 真实7-DoF eye-to-hand系统上，30/30次试验角点RMSE降至初始值的≤10%；最终有效帧保持该判据25/30（83.33%）。消融去掉future-error对齐后保持率降至26.67%。学习到的进度信号与未参与训练/控制的AprilTag外部角点误差一致（平均Spearman ρ=0.8778）。不重训即可迁移到两个未见3D目标，平移误差下降86.48%与90.27%，旋转误差下降70.01%与65.70%。

Conclusion: 将世界模型的预测目标从"动作合理性"转为"进度/误差收缩对齐"，可支撑可重复的闭环纠偏并带来零样本目标迁移；代码与数据将开源。

Abstract: Closed-loop visual servoing requires predictions that indicate whether an action reduces task error, not only whether the action is plausible. We call this gap the prediction-control mismatch and introduce WM-VS, a target-centric progress-aligned world-model framework for closed-loop visual servoing. Offline target-region DINOv2 correspondences define a signed four-dimensional servo coordinate for translation, scale, and in-plane rotation. Stage 1 aligns action-conditioned latent transitions with this coordinate; Stage 2 freezes the world model and trains a reactive joint-velocity policy with action imitation, consequence supervision, and short imagined rollouts that favor error contraction. Deployment is RGB-only and reactive, without online trajectory optimization. On a real 7-DoF eye-to-hand system, WM-VS reaches a corner RMSE no larger than 10 percent of its initial value in 30/30 trials and retains this criterion at the final valid frame in 25/30 (83.33 percent). Removing future-error alignment reduces retention to 26.67 percent. The learned progress signal agrees with an external AprilTag corner error not used for training or control (mean Spearman rho = 0.8778). Without retraining, two unseen 3D targets achieve translation-error reductions of 86.48 percent and 90.27 percent and rotation-error reductions of 70.01 percent and 65.70 percent. These results link progress-aligned action consequences to repeated closed-loop correction and transfer. Code and data will be released as open source.

</details>


### [76] [AeRove: A Compact Bimodal Aerial-Terrestrial Drone with Rapid Bistable Reconfiguration for Close-Range Pipeline Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20965)
*Caleb Polillio,Petras Swissler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出紧凑型双模态空地机器人 AeRove，利用螺旋桨护罩作滚轮沿管道滚动，通过弹簧加载双稳态机构在 200 ms 内完成地面/飞行模式切换且无需持续驱动功率，从而将巡检功耗降低 14 倍并大幅延长行程，同时利用桨流增强气体检测，在钢管上完成自主巡检与泄漏检测实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近距管道巡检对标准无人机而言存在悬停功耗高、气流敏感以及螺旋桨尾流干扰气体传感等问题；作者希望以“以滚代飞”的方式降低能耗、提升续航与传感质量。

Method: 设计双模态空中-地面机器人 AeRove：以螺旋桨护罩作为滚动轮实现管道地面行进；采用弹簧加载双稳态机构，在 200 ms 内完成地面与飞行模式的重构，且两种状态均无需持续执行器功率维持；护罩收敛几何提升推力效率；飞行仅用于越障跳跃，连续通行靠地面滚动；采用机腹桨流进气构型增强气体响应，并与外伸探头对比。

Result: 相同条件下电流由 10 A 降至 0.7 A（14 倍降幅），估计行程由 144 m 提升至 2057 m；在直径 51 cm 钢管上自主完成直线段与弯曲段导航、越障跳跃及模拟标记泄漏检测；CO₂ 感知实验中，停栖状态响应显著大于悬停；飞行时机腹桨流进气构型比外伸探头响应更快更强。

Conclusion: 将滚动作为主要通行方式、飞行仅用于越障，并结合桨流辅助气体采样，可在近距管道巡检中同时改善能效、行程与气体检测性能，为受限空间巡检提供可行方案。

Abstract: Close-proximity pipeline inspection is challenging for standard drones due to high hovering power consumption, airflow sensitivity, and propeller wash interference with gas sensing. To address this, we present AeRove, a compact bimodal aerial-terrestrial robot. AeRove uses its propeller guards as wheels to roll along pipes and employs a spring-loaded bistable mechanism to reconfigure between ground and flight modes in 200 ms without continuous actuator power to maintain either state. The converging propeller-guard geometry also improves measured thrust efficiency. By reserving flight for obstacle hopping and using ground rolling for continuous traversal, current draw is reduced 14x compared to continuous flight (0.7 A vs. 10 A), extending estimated travel distance from 144 m to 2057 m. Autonomous trials on a 51 cm diameter steel pipe demonstrated navigation on straight and curved sections, obstacle jumping, and leak detection of simulated inspection markers. Separate CO2 sensing experiments evaluated gas-detection performance under perched and aerial operating conditions. Perched inspection produced a substantially larger concentration response than hovering under the tested conditions. During flight, an underbody propeller-intake configuration produced a faster and stronger gas-detection response than an extended probe by leveraging propeller-induced airflow. Code and designs are released under the CC-BY license.

</details>


### [77] [Shake to Learn: Dynamic Interrogation of Hidden Object Physics for Robotic Manipulation with Physical Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20970)
*Wen Sin Lor,Jun Wang,Suyi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出"摇动学习"（shake-to-learn）范式：以折纸启发软体机械臂作为物理储备池计算机，通过固定摇动激励捕获耦合系统的衰减响应，从中解码隐藏物体物理属性（质心方向、距离），并可指导重抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多与操作相关的物理属性（如质心位置、内部填充）无法通过视觉直接观测，只在物体被抬升、摇动等动态扰动时才显现。作者希望将这种交互诱导的动态响应转化为一种新的机器人感知模态，用于推断传统传感无法获得的物体物理量。

Method: 构建折纸式软体机械臂物理储备池计算机；抓取物体后在基座施加固定摇动激励，通过相机追踪或嵌入式传感器记录衰减（ringdown）响应；由于输入在各试验间保持恒定，隐藏物体属性通过对"机器人-物体"耦合动力学的影响被编码，再用轻量线性读出器解码；进一步提出衰减响应的动态摘要表示以提升预测精度。

Result: 在三个难度递增的任务上完成验证：(1) 推断隐藏质心的方向；(2) 推断质心到抓取点的距离；(3) 利用推断的质心信息指导后续重抓取。动态摘要表示进一步提高了预测准确率。

Conclusion: 摇动式机械询问可作为一种有效策略，使机器人系统把短暂的物理交互转化为关于隐藏物体属性的可操作线索，服务于下游操作任务。该方法可解释、读出轻量，但真机验证规模与物体多样性仍有限。

Abstract: Many physical properties relevant to robotic manipulation are hidden from vision. A sealed object, for example, may reveal little about its center of mass (COM) or internal contents until it is lifted, shaken, or otherwise dynamically perturbed. This study shows that such interactions can enable a new modality of robotic perception and learning, in which interaction-induced dynamic responses are used to infer object physics that is inaccessible to conventional sensing. We implement this idea using an origami-inspired soft robotic arm that functions as a physical reservoir computer. After grasping an object, the arm is excited by a fixed shaking input at its base, and the resulting ringdown response is recorded through either camera tracking or embedded sensors. Because the input is held constant across trials, hidden object properties, such as the COM position, are encoded through their effect on the dynamics of the coupled robot-object system. A lightweight linear readout can then decode these dynamics to recover interpretable information about the hidden object physics. Using this framework, the soft robotic arm reservoir completed three tasks of increasing difficulty: inferring the orientation of the object's hidden COM, inferring the COM distance from the grasp point, and using the inferred COM information to guide a subsequent regrasp. We further develop a dynamic summary representation of the ringdown response that improves prediction accuracy. Together, these results establish shake-to-learn mechanical interrogation as a promising strategy for robotic systems to convert brief physical interactions into actionable cues about hidden object properties for downstream manipulation.

</details>


### [78] [ForeTac-VLA: A Forecasting-Based Tactile-Vision-Language-Action Model for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20980)
*Zhengyu Tao,Xin Li,Xin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ForeTac-VLA，一个基于触觉预测的触觉-视觉-语言融合模型，通过预测未来触觉状态来指导动作生成，在四项真实接触丰富操作任务上平均成功率达95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型依赖视觉感知，在接触丰富的环境中因关键物理交互状态不可视觉观测而鲁棒性不足；已有触觉增强VLA多使用观测到的触觉反馈，属于被动反应式，未显式建模接触如何演变。

Method: 将近期触觉观测编码为时序表示，通过双向交叉注意力与视觉-语言特征融合；用基于Transformer的预测模块预测多步未来触觉状态，使模型能对已观测与预期接触联合推理；融合的多模态表示与预测触觉状态输入VLA主干以条件化动作生成；采用真值到预测的课程学习稳定训练。

Result: 在四个真实接触丰富操作任务上平均成功率95%，比微调VLA模型高36.25个百分点，比SOTA触觉增强VLA基线高22个百分点以上；在低光照和视觉杂乱条件下仍保持强性能。

Conclusion: 通过显式预测未来触觉状态并将观测与预期接触联合推理，可显著提升接触丰富操作任务中VLA的鲁棒性与成功率，为触觉增强的具身策略提供了新范式。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, yet their reliance on visual perception limits robustness in contact-rich environments, where critical physical interaction states may not be visually observable. Existing tactile-enhanced VLA methods improve physical grounding using observed tactile feedback, but most remain largely reactive rather than explicitly modeling how contact may evolve. Therefore, we propose ForeTac-VLA, a forecasting-based tactile-vision-language fusion model that predicts future tactile states to guide action generation. Specifically, ForeTac-VLA encodes recent tactile observations into temporal representations and integrates them with vision-language features through bidirectional cross-attention. Further, a transformer-based forecasting module predicts multi-step future tactile states, enabling the model to reason jointly over observed and anticipated contact. Finally, the fused multimodal representations and predicted future tactile states are fed into the VLA backbone to condition action generation. To stabilize training, a ground-truth-to-prediction curriculum is employed when early forecasts are unreliable. Across four real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ForeTac-VLA achieves an average success rate of 95%, outperforming the fine-tuned VLA model by 36.25 percentage points and state-of-the-art tactile-enhanced VLA baselines by over 22 percentage points. ForeTac-VLA also maintains strong performance under low-illumination and visually cluttered conditions. Video demonstrations can be found on this https URL

</details>


### [79] [PIVOT: Physically Informed Vision-Language Off-Road Traversability for Field Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20983)
*Aoran Jiao,Wenda Zhao,Hshmat Sahak,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PIVOT 将 VLM 语义推理与几何规划结合用于越野可通行性评估，用真实测量标定 VLM 预测（能量、振动、滑移）并加权融合成统一得分，配合两层导航架构（几何优先、语义按需重规划），在 6.4 km 混合地形闭环试验中将自主率从 59.6% 提升至 97.0%，人工干预 11→3 次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野机器人需要可通行性评估。传统几何方法计算快但在非结构化环境中过于保守，导致频繁失败与人工干预；而单纯引入 VLM 语义判断又缺乏物理依据，难以量化其可信度。作者希望兼顾几何规划的效率与语义推理的泛化能力，并用真实测量为 VLM 输出"落地"。

Method: 1) 物理信息化的语义评估：用真实世界测量标定 VLM 预测的通行能耗、机器人振动、车轮滑移三模态，按各模态"预测-测量相关性"加权，构建统一可通行性得分。2) 两层导航架构：以几何规划为标称模式，仅当几何模式找不到路径时才触发语义重规划，控制 VLM 调用开销。

Result: 在约 6.4 km 混合地形路线上进行 5 次重复闭环试验：整体自主率 59.6%→97.0%，人工干预 11→3 次，平均干预间隔（MDBI）由 69.2 m 提升至 412.9 m（相对几何-only 基线）。

Conclusion: 经物理标定的 VLM 地形评估能显著突破几何方法的自主性上限，同时以几何规划作为标称模式保持计算效率；该"几何+按需语义"范式对野外机器人导航是可行的工程路径。

Abstract: Terrain assessment is a critical capability for off-road mobile robots, enabling safe and reliable navigation through unstructured and geometrically complex environments. Conventional geometry-based terrain assessment is fast to compute but often overly conservative in unstructured environments. We present PIVOT: a Physically Informed Vision-Language Off-Road Traversability navigation system that augments conventional geometry-based planning with vision-language-model (VLM)-based semantic reasoning for field robots. To physically ground this assessment, we quantify how strongly the VLM's predicted traversal energy cost, robot vibration, and wheel slip correlate with real-world measurements and introduce a unified traversability score that weights each modality by its prediction-measurement correlation. For efficiency, we design a two-level navigation architecture that retains geometry-based planning as the nominal mode and invokes semantic replanning only when that mode fails to find a path. Across five repeated closed-loop trials on a mixed-terrain route totalling around $6.4$ km, the proposed system increases overall autonomy from $59.6\%$ to $97.0\%$, reduces human interventions from $11$ to $3$, and increases the mean distance between interventions (MDBI) from $69.2$ m to $412.9$ m compared with geometry-only navigation. These results demonstrate that physically grounded VLM-based terrain assessment can substantially extend autonomous navigation beyond the limitations of geometry alone, while preserving efficient geometric planning as the nominal mode.

</details>


### [80] [Do Spinning Radar Doppler Velocity Measurements Improve Vehicle Detection and Tracking?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21000)
*Eric Xie,Daniil Lisus,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究旋转式FMCW雷达的多普勒速度测量是否提升车辆检测与跟踪性能，并提出自动标注流程与多普勒速度先验方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转FMCW雷达在自动驾驶感知中因抗恶劣天气和360°视野而受青睐，且新型扫描雷达可输出逐方位角多普勒速度。但多普勒速度测量对检测与跟踪的实际增益尚未被系统验证，且缺乏带真值动态物体标注的多普勒旋转雷达数据集。

Method: 检测端：估计自车运动，对雷达图像做多普勒距离畸变校正（undistortion）后再输入网络。跟踪端：提出逐车辆速度估计方法，作为跟踪器运动模型的先验。数据端：构建自动标注流水线，用集成微调的现成LiDAR检测器标注Boreas Road Trip数据集全部643 km，将检测迁移到雷达，取超过250 km车辆密集序列作为真值训练数据。

Result: 多普勒畸变校正使检测mAP最多提升2.37个点；多普勒速度先验相比零速度初始化基线，将MOTA提升13.68个点，并达到使用真值速度先验所得MOTA的99.7%。

Conclusion: 多普勒速度测量能显著改善旋转雷达车辆检测与跟踪，所提畸变校正与速度先验方法有效，且自动标注流水线可缓解多普勒雷达数据缺乏动态标注的问题。

Abstract: Spinning frequency-modulated continuous-wave (FMCW) radars have been gaining popularity in autonomous vehicle perception on account of their robustness to adverse weather conditions and 360° field of view. Recently, scanning radars have also been shown capable of generating per-azimuth Doppler velocity. In this paper, we investigate whether these Doppler velocity measurements improve spinning radar vehicle detection and tracking performance. For detection, we estimate the ego motion and use it to undo the Doppler range distortion of the radar image before passing it to a network. For tracking, we propose a new way to estimate a per-vehicle velocity and use it as a prior for the tracker's motion model. Since Doppler-enabled spinning radar data is not available in any dataset with ground-truth dynamic object labels, our first contribution is an automatic labelling pipeline that uses an ensemble of fine-tuned off-the-shelf lidar detectors to label all 643 km of the Boreas Road Trip dataset. We then transfer detections to radar, and use over 250 km of vehicle-dense sequences as ground-truth training data. By training and evaluating two state-of-the-art detectors, we show that Doppler undistortion can improve detection accuracy by up to $2.37$ points on mean average precision. Furthermore, we show that the Doppler velocity prior can improve tracking accuracy by $13.68$ points on multi-object tracking accuracy (MOTA) versus the zero-velocity initialization baseline, while achieving $99.7\%$ of the MOTA obtained using ground-truth velocities as the prior.

</details>


### [81] [Project SCOUT: Interceptor Drone for Perimeter Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21005)
*Azmain Yousuf,Siwei Cai,Knut Peterson,Lifeng Zhou,David Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SCOUT：一种ROS集成的机载感知与控制框架，用TensorRT检测+ByteTrack关联+轨迹保持状态机实现无人机防御的实时视觉跟踪与闭环拦截控制，实飞中92.2%帧有效检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非法无人机增多，需要抗干扰的周界防御反无人机系统；现有方案多依赖外部传感或地面站算力，缺乏纯机载实时感知-控制闭环的可行性验证。

Method: 完全机载的ROS集成框架：TensorRT加速的无人机视觉检测、ByteTrack数据关联、轻量级轨迹保持状态机（抑制目标跳变、维持短时检测中断下的连续性），并生成控制指令；以平面“守门员”拦截策略进行硬件实飞验证，防御机追踪来袭目标并维持边界附近的封堵构型。

Result: 真实飞行实验中，SCOUT在实时机载检测速率下保持92.2%帧的有效目标检测率，验证了纯机载视觉跟踪与闭环控制在无人机周界防御中的可行性，并提供端到端演示视频。

Conclusion: 结果证明无需外部感知基础设施或地面计算的机载感知-控制闭环可支撑反无人机周界防御，轨迹保持状态机是提升控制稳定性的关键组件；未来可扩展到三维拦截与更复杂机动目标。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of unauthorized unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) has created a growing need for robust, jamming-resistant counter-UAV systems for perimeter defense. This paper presents \textbf{SCOUT} (Spatial Computation for Optimized UAV Tracking), a ROS-integrated onboard perception and control framework for real-time aerial defense against incoming UAVs. SCOUT performs visual detection, target association, track filtering, and control command generation directly onboard the defender UAV, without relying on external sensing infrastructure or ground-station computation. To provide stable control inputs, the perception pipeline combines TensorRT-accelerated drone detection with ByteTrack-based association and a lightweight track-retention state machine. The state machine rejects abrupt target jumps and maintains short-term target continuity during temporary detection degradation, reducing unstable control responses caused by false detections or target switching. We evaluate the proposed architecture through an integrated hardware deployment executing a planar ``goalkeeping'' interception strategy. In this setting, the defender UAV tracks the incoming target and adjusts its motion to maintain a blocking configuration near the protected boundary. Real-world flight results show that SCOUT maintains valid target detections for 92.2\% of frames while operating at real-time onboard detection rates, demonstrating the feasibility of visual tracking and closed-loop control for UAV perimeter defense. A video demonstration of the end-to-end perimeter defense operation is available online. this https URL

</details>


### [82] [SPARROW: Survival-POMCP for Adaptive Robot Routing, Observation, and Waiting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21008)
*Hshmat Sahak,Aoran Jiao,Nicholas Rhinehart,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SPARROW：将含临时障碍的图导航建模为部分可观测半马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），基于POMCP在信念空间上同时搜索通行、观测与有限时长等待动作，并在线学习含右删失数据的类别条件生存模型，用价值-of-learning准则权衡数据采集成本与未来导航后悔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在规划路线上遇到临时障碍（如短暂占用通道）时，需要在等待、绕行、先获取更多障碍信息之间做序贯决策。已有方法（如OSCAR）多用生存模型处理等待/绕行取舍，但缺乏对"主动观测以降低不确定性"的显式建模，也没有在线利用绕行导致的右删失清障数据，因而决策次优、到达目标耗时偏长。

Method: 1）问题形式化：图导航在临时障碍下建模为部分可观测半马尔可夫决策过程（POSMDP），隐状态为障碍类别与清障时间，动作为通行、观测、有限时长等待。2）SPARROW：基于POMCP的信念空间规划器，维护对潜在障碍类别与清障时间的粒子信念。3）在线学习类别条件生存模型，同时使用已观测清障事件与右删失遭遇（机器人在观测到清障前已绕行）。4）生成式模型随动作展开模拟障碍到达与清障，使规划能考虑替代路径上可能出现的新阻塞。5）引入value-of-learning准则，权衡采集带标签生存数据的即时代价与其对未来导航后悔的期望降低。

Result: 在两个仿真图与多种障碍类别设置下，SPARROW相对同问题的近期生存模型方法OSCAR将平均到达目标时间降低12–26%。在真实移动机器人上，相对OSCAR平均到达目标时间降低20.5%，并能随环境条件变化自适应地选择观测、等待与绕行。

Conclusion: SPARROW表明，将临时障碍导航显式建模为含主动观测与等待的信念空间规划问题，并在线融合右删失生存数据与价值-of-learning准则，可显著提升移动机器人在动态阻塞环境中的导航效率，且方法可迁移到真实平台。

Abstract: Temporary obstacles that may block a robot's planned route create a sequential navigation problem: a robot must decide whether to wait for a blockage to clear, reroute, or acquire more information about the obstacle before acting. We formulate graph navigation among temporary obstacles as a partially observable semi-Markov decision process and introduce SPARROW, a belief-space planner built on Partially Observable Monte Carlo Planning (POMCP). SPARROW searches over traversal, observation, and finite-duration waiting actions while maintaining a particle belief over latent obstacle classes and clearance times. Class-conditioned survival models are learned online from both clearance observations and right-censored encounters where the robot reroutes before clearance is observed. A generative model simulates obstacle arrivals and clearances as each action unfolds, so the planner can account for blockages that may occur along alternative routes. We further introduce a value-of-learning criterion that trades the immediate cost of collecting labelled survival data against its expected reduction in future navigation regret. Across two simulation graphs and multiple obstacle-class settings, SPARROW reduces mean time-to-goal by 12-26% relative to OSCAR, a recent survival-based method for the same problem. On a physical mobile robot, SPARROW reduces mean time-to-goal by 20.5% relative to OSCAR while selectively observing, waiting, and rerouting as environment conditions change.

</details>


### [83] [Towards Effective Visual-Inertial SLAM with Passive-Only Sensors for Low-Cost Autonomous Underwater Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21015)
*Grant Schwidder,David Widhalm,Junaed Sattar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 用不到1万美元的消费级传感器平台（立体相机、MEMS IMU、深度传感器）在水下6-DOF无约束环境中评估开源VI-SLAM方案，并提出传感器融合优化，证明低成本AUV也可获得可用的SLAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高端AUV依赖DVL、USBL等昂贵传感器，导致先进海洋机器人难以从专业实验室推广到普通研究与爱好者群体，需要验证低成本平台能否实现鲁棒导航。

Method: 基于约1万美元价位的开源AUV，仅使用消费级立体相机、MEMS IMU和深度传感器，在完全无约束的6-DOF水下环境中测试现成SLAM包，并针对无缆水下作业的视觉与物理挑战提出传感器融合优化。

Result: 证明在此低成本配置下可获得可用的SLAM解，为苛刻的实时海洋任务提供了性能基准预期。

Conclusion: 高质量水下SLAM不必依赖工业级硬件，低成本消费级方案可使SLAM“面向大众”普及。

Abstract: Improvements to Visual-Inertial Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (VI-SLAM) for low-cost autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) are critical for transitioning advanced marine robotics from specialized labs to broader research and hobbyist applications. While high-end AUVs typically rely on expensive sensor suites - such as Doppler Velocity Logs (DVLs) and Ultra-Short Baseline (USBL) systems - this work demonstrates that robust, high-quality navigation is achievable using a sub-$10, 000(USD) platform equipped only with inexpensive consumer-grade sensors. By leveraging a similarly priced, open-source AUV, we evaluate the performance of stereo cameras, Micro-electromechanical System (MEMS)-based IMUs, and depth sensors in a fully unconstrained 6-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) underwater environment. We analyze the efficacy of off-the-shelf SLAM packages and propose optimizations for sensor fusion to mitigate the visual and physical challenges of untethered underwater operation. Our results prove that a usable SLAM solution can be accessible to the masses, providing a benchmark for expectations in demanding, real-time maritime missions without the financial barrier of industrial-grade hardware.

</details>


### [84] [Catch Me If You Can: Real-Time Feedback Denoising for Responsive VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21022)
*Yiheng Ji,Xingru Zhou,Luis Sentis,Mingyo Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-Feedback：双时间尺度架构，将扩散动作生成器的最后一步去噪保留为高频视觉反馈接口，实现执行前的实时动作校正，动态任务成功率大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散式VLA的动作块通常在推理后开环执行，物体移动、接触变化或场景演化时响应性差；而重跑完整视觉-语言扩散模型代价过高。动机在于兼顾扩散规划的时序连贯表达力与实时闭环校正能力。

Method: 提出VLA-Feedback，低频扩散规划与高频视觉反馈结合的双时间尺度架构。不完整去噪动作块再执行，而是保留最终去噪步作为轻量反馈接口，执行每个动作前用最新观测进行校正；无需重跑完整VLA扩散模型即可保留扩散规划器的表达力并实现实时修正。

Result: 静态LIBERO任务上与GR00T持平；动态仿真任务平均成功率从27.5%提升至85.0%；真机任务平均成功率从51%提升至73%。

Conclusion: 将扩散去噪末步复用为反馈接口是一种低开销、有效的VLA闭环执行范式，在动态场景与真机部署中均取得显著增益，为扩散式VLA的实时响应性问题提供了实用解法。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong generalization in robotic manipulation by combining semantic knowledge from pretrained vision-language models with expressive action-generation policies. Diffusion-based action generators are particularly effective for modeling temporally coherent action chunks, but these chunks are typically executed open-loop after inference. This limits responsiveness when objects move, contacts change, or the scene evolves during execution. We propose VLA-Feedback, a two-timescale architecture that combines low-frequency diffusion planning with high-frequency visual feedback. Rather than fully denoising an action chunk before execution, VLA-Feedback retains its final denoising step as a lightweight feedback interface, allowing each action to be corrected using the latest observation before it is executed. This design preserves the expressiveness of the diffusion planner while enabling real-time action correction without rerunning the full vision-language diffusion model. VLA-Feedback matched GR00T on static LIBERO tasks while improving average success on dynamic simulation tasks from 27.5% to 85.0%. On real-robot tasks, it improved average success from 51% to 73%. Additional materials can be found on our project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [85] [Adapting Rigid-Body Dynamics Derivatives for Constraint Embedding Closed-Chain Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21024)
*Daniel J. Volpi,Patrick M. Wensing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将刚体动力学一阶导数的现有高效算法推广到基于约束嵌入建模的闭链系统，去除"关节速度效应局部构型不变"假设，推导适用于更一般关节类型的动力学导数算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效动力学导数方法假定关节速度效应满足局部构型不变性，该假设对包含闭链的约束嵌入驱动模型不成立，限制了模型预测控制与可微仿真中动力学计算的精度。

Method: 在约束嵌入框架下重新推导刚体动力学一阶导数算法，去除局部构型不变假设，得到可处理更一般关节类型（含约束嵌入闭链）的适配算法；对比传统销轴关节模型与刻画局部闭链的完整驱动模型。

Result: 推广引入的附加项在仅建模驱动运动学时代价很低；但当驱动链中包含额外刚体（如电机转子）或考虑非局部闭链时，计算开销显著上升。

Conclusion: 该工作使约束嵌入驱动子机构可获得更精确的动力学计算，可被模型预测控制与可微仿真采纳。

Abstract: This paper extends an existing algorithm for the first-order derivatives of rigid-body dynamics to the case of closed-chain kinematic systems modeled using constraint embed- ding. Many standard dynamics algorithms apply to both open- chain and constraint-embedded models, but existing efficient derivative methods assume joint velocity effects are locally config- uration invariant. We remove this assumption and derive adapted algorithms that extend dynamics derivatives to more general joint types, including those arising in constraint-embedded closed- chain models. Our results compare conventional pin-joint robot models with more complete actuation models that capture local closed chains. We show that the additional terms introduced by these generalizations have low computational impact when mod- eling actuation kinematics alone, but can incur higher cost when additional rigid bodies, such as motor rotors, are included in the actuation chain, or when considering non-local loops. Overall, these results enable more accurate dynamics computations for constraint-embedded actuation submechanisms to be adopted in model-predictive control and differentiable simulation.

</details>


### [86] [MAPLE-RF: Efficient Probabilistic RF Source Localization in Partially Explored Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21026)
*Haozhe Lei,Sundeep Rangan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在部分探索地图下比较单快照射频发射源定位的两类后验输出方法：扩展数字孪生射线追踪法与MAPLE-RF（U-Net单次打分），后者推理成本比全网格通用射线追踪低两个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在SLAM探索中仅有部分地图，而RF源定位通常依赖完整环境模型预测遮挡与反射，因此需要研究部分已知地图条件下如何输出发射源位置后验。

Method: 将数字孪生方法扩展至部分地图：把未探索区域视为自由空间，并在混合覆盖度地图上训练；MAPLE-RF则把估计的到达路径角与信噪比编码为与地图已知度、占据、视距可见性对齐的网格通道，用U-Net一次前向为所有候选位置打分，推理时不做传播仿真。基于室内环境的射线追踪仿真进行对比，并用于自身估计引导的探索路径。

Result: 混合覆盖度训练对两种方法都必要；数字孪生法在多数单快照指标上更准，MAPLE-RF接近其精度且查询代价与传播模型无关，比全新全网格通用射线追踪低两个数量级以上；两者均优于高斯与高斯混合基线；在自主引导探索路径上，融合的MAPLE-RF后验在源附近概率更高。

Conclusion: 部分地图下的RF源定位需混合覆盖训练；学习式单次打分方法以极低推理开销逼近数字孪生精度，并更适合在线探索中的重复查询。

Abstract: Localizing a radio-frequency (RF) transmitter from received signals often requires a model of the environment to predict how obstacles block and reflect the signal. In many robotic applications, however, only a partial map is available, particularly when a robot localizes the source while exploring with simultaneous localization and mapping (SLAM). We study single-snapshot transmitter localization on such partially explored maps and compare two approaches that output a posterior over transmitter locations. The first extends a digital-twin method, which ray-traces every candidate location, to partial maps by treating unexplored space as free and training on mixed map coverage. The second, MAPLE-RF, encodes estimated path angles of arrival and signal-to-noise ratios as grid channels aligned with map knownness, occupancy, and line-of-sight visibility, and a U-Net scores all candidate positions in one pass without simulating propagation at inference. Ray-tracing simulations of indoor environments indicate that training on mixed map coverage is essential for both approaches. The digital-twin approach is more accurate on most single-snapshot metrics, while MAPLE-RF comes close at a query cost that does not depend on the propagation model and is more than two orders of magnitude below a fresh full-grid query with general-purpose ray tracing. Both outperform Gaussian and Gaussian-mixture baselines, and on exploration routes guided by its own estimates, fused MAPLE-RF posteriors place more probability near the source than the compared methods. Code and data will be released.

</details>


### [87] [DEXTERA: From a Single Image to Deployable Dexterous Manipulation via Real-to-Sim-to-Real](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21045)
*Jin Wu,Lianjie Yuan,Zeyan Sun,Yuanyuan Lei,Disi A,Bicheng Han,Fangzhou Xia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DEXTERA 提出自动化 real-to-sim-to-real 框架，从单张 RGB 图像构建可部署的灵巧操作策略，涵盖场景分解、度量对齐与机器人标定、任务基元与轨迹合成、统一多模态策略接口四阶段，并验证零样本与协同训练部署效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人灵巧操作数据采集成本高、耗时长，高保真仿真虽可扩展但数字孪生构建依赖人工，且视觉、几何与动力学残差导致 sim-to-real 迁移不可靠。

Method: 四阶段统一流水线：(1) 单图场景分解为静态 Gaussian 背景与可交互刚体/铰接资产，并由 VLM 推断物理参数；(2) 度量级场景全局对齐、物体规范化与形态平衡的机器人标定；(3) 可扩展仿真任务基元构建、VR 遥操作与以物体为中心轨迹合成；(4) 共享多模态策略接口，同时支持模仿学习与强化学习。

Result: 在 13 个任务-本体对、2 个灵巧机器人平台、6 种策略架构上评测；视觉保真度与 3D 几何重建优于生成式基线，跨域轨迹回放验证物理交互一致性；纯仿真训练策略可实现零样本真机部署，仿真-真实协同训练将平均物理策略成功率由 29.2% 提升至 61.9%。

Conclusion: 单图驱动的自动化 real-to-sim-to-real 管线可显著降低数字孪生构建成本并提升跨域策略迁移成功率，为灵巧操作的可扩展数据合成与部署提供统一方案。

Abstract: Collecting real-world robot data for dexterous manipulation is costly and time-consuming. While high-fidelity physics simulators enable scalable data synthesis and policy learning, constructing deployment-ready digital twins manually remains labor-intensive, and residual visual, geometric, and dynamics gaps hinder reliable sim-to-real transfer. We present DEXTERA, an automated real-to-sim-to-real framework that transforms a single RGB image into deployable policies for dexterous manipulation across four unified stages: (1) single-image scene factorization into a static Gaussian background and interactive rigid or articulated assets with VLM-inferred physical parameters; (2) metric scene global alignment, object canonicalization, and morphology-balanced robot calibration; (3) scalable simulator task primitive construction, VR teleoperation, and object-centric trajectory synthesis; and (4) a shared multimodal policy interface supporting both imitation learning and reinforcement learning. We evaluate DEXTERA across 13 task-embodiment pairs, 2 dexterous robot platforms, and 6 policy architectures. Experimental results demonstrate that DEXTERA achieves superior visual fidelity and 3D geometric reconstruction compared to generative baselines, while cross-domain trajectory replays validate strong physical interaction consistency. Furthermore, simulation-only trained policies enable viable zero-shot real-robot deployment, while simulation-real co-training substantially improves mean physical policy success from 29.2% to 61.9% across diverse policy architectures.

</details>


### [88] [Constraint-Unified MPC for Over-Actuated Surface Vehicles with Post-Detection Fault Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21046)
*Sebastian Burmester,Ruiheng Jiang,Noa Sendlhofer,Raffaello D'Andrea,Aswin Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将轨迹跟踪、推力分配与单推进器力/速率限幅统一为一个二次规划（QP）的控制架构，故障重构仅通过外部检测器提供的每推进器二值标志进入。系统在苏黎世湖实景演出与威尼斯2025展览中部署，实测10分钟定点RMSE 1.6 cm，10 m方形轨迹0.6 m/s下RMSE 4.3 cm；单推进器失效误差升至约11.6 cm，双推进器失效仅能以1.25 m RMSE 跟踪0.4 m/s方形。相比级联基线，统一式在相同厘米级精度下推力约为一半，且故障后仍保持在参考路径24 cm内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水上编队表演要求小型自主水面艇在推进器推力与速率受限条件下精确跟踪路径，并需在推进器故障后维持可控性。现有级联式控制将跟踪、分配与限幅分层处理，约束传递不一致，故障后易偏离参考路径，因此需要一个统一处理约束与故障重构的实时控制器。

Method: 将轨迹跟踪、推力分配以及每推进器的力与速率限制统一建模为单个关于推进器指令的二次规划，约束在同一优化层内同时满足；故障重构不引入额外模式切换，仅由外部故障检测器输出的每推进器二值标志改变QP中的可行域/约束。系统为已部署的实船控制栈，并在与自研级联基线对比中评估标称与故障场景。

Result: 实船现场实验：10分钟定点 RMSE 1.6 cm；10 m方形轨迹、0.6 m/s 下 RMSE 4.3 cm。0.5 m/s 时前推进器失效误差 11.6 cm，右舷推进器失效 11.5 cm；同时两个推进器失效时以 0.4 m/s 跟踪方形，RMSE 1.25 m。与级联基线相比，统一式标称方形精度相当（厘米级）、定点保持更紧且推力约为其一半；故障后统一控制器偏离参考路径不超过 24 cm，而级联控制器离开参考路径。

Conclusion: 将跟踪、分配与执行器限幅合并为单一QP、并以每推进器二值标志注入故障重构，可在真实演出级部署中实现厘米级轨迹精度、显著降低推力消耗并提升推进器故障后的鲁棒性；双推进器同时失效仍是能力边界，误差达米级。

Abstract: Choreographed aquatic performances require small autonomous surface vehicles to track precise paths under per-thruster force and rate limits, including after thruster failures. We report a deployed system in which trajectory tracking, thrust allocation, and the per-thruster force and rate limits are resolved in a single quadratic program over the per-thruster commands, with fault reconfiguration entering through one binary flag per thruster from an external detector. The system has driven a fleet in live performances on Lake Zürich and at the Time Space Existence 2025 exhibition in Venice. The field campaign measures 1.6 cm root mean square position error in a 10-minute hold and 4.3 cm over a 10 m square at 0.6 m/s. At 0.5 m/s, losing the front thruster increases the error to 11.6 cm, while losing the starboard-side thruster increases it to 11.5 cm. Losing two thrusters simultaneously leaves the craft tracking a 0.4 m/s square with a root mean square position error of 1.25 m. Compared to our own cascaded baseline, the unified formulation tracks the nominal square to the same few centimeters and holds station more tightly with roughly half the thruster force. The architectures separate after a thruster failure, where the unified controller stays within 24 cm of the reference path while the cascade leaves it.

</details>


### [89] [PlantShade: Predicting Plant Shadows for Lighting-Aware Robotic Agricultural Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21059)
*Longchao Da,Xiaoou Liu,Xingjian Li,Lirong Xiang,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文构建了覆盖大豆、番茄、甜菜、草莓四种作物的植物生长与阴影数据集（顶部视角+环形光源轨迹，多生长阶段），并提出基于扩散模型的生成式阴影模拟方法，引入时间条件以支持跨生长阶段的动态阴影生成，可服务于感知、光照控制和视角规划等下游机器人任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物生长与农业生产是可持续发展与民生基础，而阴影（遮蔽）是影响植物生长的关键因素。当前缺乏系统的植物阴影数据与可泛化的阴影模拟方法，作者希望推动"植物感知的农业智能"，并服务于农业机器人下游任务。

Method: 1) 构建植物生长与阴影数据集：4种作物（大豆、番茄、甜菜、草莓），顶部俯视视角，辅以沿圆形轨迹运动的光源，在多个生长阶段产生动态阴影，覆盖多样化观测复杂度；2) 提出基于扩散模型的生成式阴影模拟，加入时间条件（temporal conditioning）以支持不同生长阶段的灵活阴影生成，并面向未见植物泛化。

Result: 论文进行了定量与定性评估以验证模型性能，声称可对未见植物生成较真实的阴影，并支持感知、光照控制、视角规划等下游机器人任务。摘要未给出具体数值指标、基线对比或真机验证结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为"植物感知的阴影建模"提供了基础性数据集与生成式方法，对农业与机器人应用具有潜在意义，但方法细节、量化增益与真实场景验证仍待正文呈现。

Abstract: Plant growth and agricultural production form the foundation of a country's sustainable development and directly impact human livelihoods. Recent advances in frontier artificial intelligence have enabled scientific agriculture with strong potential to improve crop productivity. In this paper, we identify the importance and inherent complexity of plant shade simulation, as shading is a critical factor influencing plant growth. To advance this field and promote broader societal benefits, we focus on two main contributions. First, we introduce a comprehensive plant growth and shade dataset covering four plant species, including soybean, tomato, sugarbeet, and strawberry. The dataset includes top-down viewpoints with a supplementary light along a circular trajectory, casting dynamic shadows across multiple growth stages and diverse observation complexities. Second, we propose generative shade simulation based on diffusion models, enabling realistic shade generation for unseen plants and supporting downstream robotic tasks such as perception, lighting control, and view planning. The model incorporates temporal conditioning to facilitate flexible shade simulation across different time stages. We conduct both quantitative and qualitative evaluations to assess model performance. This work provides a foundational study for plant-aware shade modeling and has meaningful implications for broader agricultural and robotic applications.

</details>


### [90] [Design of Adaptive PID Controller Based On Asynchronous Advantage Actor Critic Learning Method for QuadCopter Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21082)
*Ali Jokar,Aria Alasty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将A3C强化学习与PID控制器结合，用神经网络动态优化PID参数，并加入系统辨识模块预测状态；仿真显示A3C相比A2C跟踪精度相当但损失收敛与参数优化更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼具有强非线性和扰敏性，传统PID参数固定、难以应对复杂工况，需要兼具自适应性与可解释性的控制方案。

Method: 以A3C并行智能体在线整定PID增益，神经网络输出参数；配套系统辨识模块提供状态预测以支撑最优控制策略；对照标准A2C。

Result: 仿真中A3C与A2C均能准确跟踪姿态与轨迹，但A3C在奖励曲线与损失曲线上收敛更好，体现更优参数优化。

Conclusion: 强化学习与传统控制结合可提升四旋翼控制的适应性与性能，但验证仅限仿真，缺乏实机与鲁棒性证据。

Abstract: Quadcopters offer great utility in many applications, but their nonlinear nature and disturbance sensitivity present great control challenges. Basic PID controllers are generally not sophisticated enough to cope with these complexities. This paper suggests a control system that integrates the Asynchronous Advantage Actor-Critic (A3C) algorithm with a PID controller for quadcopter attitude and trajectory tracking. The A3C controller uses parallel agents to optimize PID parameters dynamically using a neural network. A system identification module for the complementary system makes predictions about system states for optimal control policy. The proposed framework was compared with a standard actor-critic (A2C) model. Simulation results verify that they both track accurately. However, the A3C-based controller converges much more for the loss function, as evidenced by reward figures and loss curves, demonstrating better parameter optimization. This shows that A3C-based approach results in improved performance for the control of quadcopter, effectively integrating reinforcement learning and traditional control to achieve higher adaptability.

</details>


### [91] [Dynamic Modeling and LQR Control of a Single Coaxial Drone with 2DOF Thrust Vectoring Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21099)
*Ali Jokar,Amin Talaeizadeh,Aria Alasty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种双自由度摆式推力矢量机构，替代斜盘实现共轴双旋翼无人机的横滚/俯仰主动控制，并建立完整拉格朗日动力学模型、LQR控制器与EKF状态估计，通过高保真仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共轴旋翼无人机节能、体积小，但横滚与俯仰通道固有欠驱动；斜盘方案虽能解决该问题却带来较大机械复杂度。因此需要一种机械上更简洁的主动推力矢量替代方案。

Method: 设计含两自由度摆式机构的共轴无人机；建立不忽略机体、舵机臂、电机组件惯性贡献的完整拉格朗日动力学模型；在悬停平衡点线性化后设计LQR控制器；基于EKF融合GPS、气压计与IMU进行状态估计；仿真中计入执行器动态与传感器噪声。

Result: 高保真仿真验证了所提架构的可行性与可靠性，EKF状态估计精度较高，表明该方案有望用于节能、快速机动的共轴无人机。

Conclusion: 摆式推力矢量机构为共轴无人机提供了一种低机械复杂度的欠驱动补偿途径，仿真层面验证有效，但尚缺乏实机实验与飞行动态验证。

Abstract: Coaxial rotor drones have generated considerable interest because of energy efficiency and small size, but they are afflicted with inherent underactuation for roll and pitch control, although systems like swashplates have circumvented this limitation at the cost of greater mechanical complexity. This work presents a novel coaxial drone supplemented by a two-degreesof-freedom pendulum mechanism for active thrust vectoring that offers a less mechanically complicated alternative. We develop a comprehensive Lagrangian dynamic model that does not ignore the inertial contributions of all the components, including body, servo arms, and motor assembly. A Linear Quadratic Regulator(LQR) is designed based on the linearized dynamics around the hover equilibrium. High-fidelity simulations taking actuator dynamics and sensor noise into account validate the proposed architecture. An Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) blends GPS, barometer, and IMU estimates with high accuracy for state estimation. The findings verify the potential and reliability of this approach for power-saving, rapid coaxial UAVs.

</details>


### [92] [Dynamics-Induced Commitment in Learning-Based Robotic Penalty Kicks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21100)
*Ruize Geng,Hao E. Zhang,Yisen Li,Yikai Wang,H. Eric Tseng,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文研究"策略信息—身体执行力"耦合，提出动力学诱导承诺映射（DIC-Map），在人形—四足点球系统中定位承诺时刻（接触前约0.29 s），给出简化零和博弈下最优策略集中的闭式界，并证明估计器介导的一阶学习通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人博弈中的学习限制不仅来自战略信息，还来自身体在给定时刻还能执行什么；作者希望显式刻画"身体能否继续执行"这一物理可行性边界如何压缩博弈的可行策略空间。

Method: 构建分层人形—四足点球系统：博弈层自博弈策略指挥固定的足球全身控制器（S-WBC）；人形射门技能由自采集动捕数据初始化，四足扑救技能用强化学习训练。提出DIC-Map：估计延续能力，识别终止备选首次持续失效的时刻，并检验剩余交互是否可归约为简化零和博弈；对对称终止备选给出由响应方延迟价值决定的最优策略集中度闭式界；进一步分析当响应方通过估计器行动时，响应价值相等会消除直接终止分配梯度，仅保留估计器介导的一阶学习通道。

Result: 实验将承诺时刻定位在接触前约0.29 s；仅改变球速即可改变可延迟覆盖范围。在四种响应方策略上，替换估计器把扑救率从0.240提升到0.472，而以等待获得的可比读数精度提升仅将其提升到0.246。

Conclusion: 承诺与延迟能力是身体约束下博弈学习的关键变量；估计器（而非单靠等待换来的读数精度）是主导扑救表现的学习通道。由于可用覆盖项只是观测代理，作者用后验分析进行均衡比较。

Abstract: Learning in robotic games is constrained not only by strategic information but also by what the body can still execute. We study this coupling in a hierarchical humanoid-quadruped penalty system in which game-level self-play policies command fixed soccer whole-body controllers (S-WBCs). The humanoid shooting skill is initialized from self-collected motion-capture data, whereas the quadruped saving skill is learned by reinforcement learning. We introduce dynamics-induced commitment mapping (DIC-Map), a body-grounded analysis that estimates continuation capability, identifies the first persistent loss of a terminal alternative, and tests whether the remaining interaction admits a reduced zero-sum game. For symmetric terminal alternatives, the reduced game yields a closed-form bound on optimal strategy concentration determined by the responder's value of deferring. We further show that, when the responder acts through an estimator, equal response values eliminate the direct terminal-allocation gradient and leave an estimator-mediated first-order learning channel. Experiments locate commitment about 0.29 s before contact, and changing only ball speed shifts deferral coverage. Across four responder policies, replacing the estimator raises save rate from 0.240 to 0.472, whereas a comparable gain in read accuracy obtained by waiting raises it only to 0.246. Posterior analysis is used for the equilibrium comparison because the available coverage terms are observational proxies. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [93] [Learning Scene-Aware Humanoid Locomotion through 3D Clutter from Immersive Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21107)
*Beichen Wang,Tong Xu,Daniel Kosukhin,Yuen-Hei Yeung,Yuanjie Lu,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MTC 框架，用 VR 程序化环境采集人类演示，经场景感知重定向生成无碰撞人形参考轨迹，训练可在 Unitree G1 上部署的场景感知全身运动策略，实现复杂杂物环境穿越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从人类运动学习的人形技能多停留在无障碍空间（舞蹈、武术），密杂约束环境中的三维全身避障穿越研究不足；且真实场景构建成本高、演示与机器人形态差异大、难以保证无碰撞。

Method: 学习自演示框架：(1) 程序化生成 VR 环境进行沉浸式数据采集，规避真实场景搭建成本；(2) 场景感知运动重定向算法，将人类演示转换为人形轨迹，并严格施加机器人-场景间隙约束以保证无碰撞；(3) 用这些参考轨迹训练场景感知运动策略，部署于 Unitree G1 人形机器人。

Result: 在自建 MTC-Challenge 多障碍穿越基准上，策略取得 70.2% 无碰撞率，能通过爬行低净空通道、挤过狭窄缝隙等多样化全身技能穿越复杂环境。

Conclusion: VR 程序化采集 + 场景感知重定向 + 策略学习的组合可有效将人类运动技能迁移到人形机器人的密杂环境导航中，为场景感知全身运动提供了可行路径。

Abstract: While learning from human motions has enabled highly dynamic humanoid skills such as dancing and martial arts in obstacle-free space, traversal through densely cluttered environments remains underexplored. These spaces are three-dimensional and geometrically constrained, requiring scene-aware locomotion that tightly couples whole-body motion with scene geometry for obstacle avoidance. To address these challenges, we present Moving Through Clutter (MTC), a learning-from-demonstration framework for scene-aware humanoid locomotion. To bypass costly physical scene construction, MTC uses procedurally generated Virtual Reality environments for immersive data collection. To transform these human motions into training-ready humanoid motions, we propose a scene-aware motion retargeting algorithm that converts human demonstrations into humanoid trajectories while strictly enforcing robot-scene clearance to guarantee collision-free traversal. These reference trajectories are then used to train a scene-aware locomotion policy that deploys on a Unitree G1 humanoid. Evaluated on our proposed MTC-Challenge for multi-obstacle traversal, the policy demonstrates a 70.2% collision-free rate across diverse scenarios, successfully traversing complex environments through diverse whole-body skills, including crawling through low-clearance passages and squeezing through narrow gaps.

</details>


### [94] [Demonstration Synthesis from a Single Scan via Gaussian Splatting for Visuomotor Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21112)
*Beichen Wang,Yuen-Hei Yeung,V.R. Sridhar Devarakonda,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaussianFactory：以单次视频扫描为输入、无需物理引擎的3DGS可编辑场景复制与任务采样数据引擎，在几何重建上纯运动学规划抓取与轨迹并渲染照片级演示，仅在抓取接触阶段引入一次预训练的接触模型，用于批量生成视觉运动策略训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略训练依赖大量与目标环境高度匹配的演示数据，而真实采集成本高昂。现有演示合成方法受限于人工投入大、视觉保真度低或对物理仿真器的强依赖，难以在视觉真实性与可扩展性之间取得平衡。

Method: 将单次视频扫描重建为可编辑的3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) 场景副本；从场景可支持的对象组合任务中采样任务；对每个任务在几何重建上以纯运动学方式规划抓取与轨迹；渲染照片级演示；仅在抓取形成阶段通过一次在交互数据集上预训练的接触模型注入接触力动力学。生成循环中不含物理引擎。

Result: 在两个场景（可复现的仿真场景与配备UR10e机械臂的真实工作空间）实现端到端scan-to-deployment流程。仅用合成演示训练的标准扩散策略分别达到95.1%与84.2%的成功率。

Conclusion: 以视频扫描为唯一人工输入、将物理动力学限制在接触决策环节的数据引擎，可在不依赖物理仿真器的前提下批量产出高保真演示，并有效支撑真实机器人上的策略部署，为具身策略的数据获取提供了可扩展路径。

Abstract: Training a visuomotor policy calls for abundant demonstrations that closely match the target environment, yet collecting them anew remains expensive. Existing demonstration synthesis methods reduce this cost but remain constrained by high manual effort, limited visual fidelity, or heavy reliance on physics simulators. This paper introduces GaussianFactory, a high-fidelity data engine that mass-produces demonstrations with a single video scan as its only human input and no physics engine in the generation loop. Specifically, GaussianFactory reconstructs the scene as an editable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) replica and samples from the object-combination tasks the scene affords. For each task, it plans grasps and trajectories purely kinematically on the geometric reconstruction, rendering photorealistic demonstrations that visually match the target environment. Physical dynamics enter the pipeline only where contact force interactions dictate the outcome---during grasp formation, via a learned contact model pretrained once on an interaction dataset. To evaluate the downstream utility of the synthesized demonstrations, we implement an end-to-end scan-to-deployment workflow in two setups: a simulated scene that stands in for the real world to enable reproducibility, and a real-world workspace with a physical UR10e robot. In each setup, a standard diffusion policy trained solely on the synthesized demonstrations achieves 95.1% and 84.2% success rates, respectively.

</details>


### [95] [Noctif3R: Feed-Forward Monocular Real-Time SLAM for Photon-Limited Scenes on Embedded Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21114)
*Mihir Chauhan,Aditya Uday Abhang,Kevin Biju Mathew,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出低光单目SLAM流水线SYS，用低光前馈pointmap前端加显式匹配门控，在高噪声黑暗环境下保持轨迹可解释性，并在Jetson AGX Orin上实现吞吐与能耗的帕累托改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在极暗环境中需用单目RGB相机实时定位，但现有方法存在断层：离线低光重建（可低至-4 dB）太慢无法实时；实时单目系统（DROID-SLAM、DPV-SLAM等）在低信噪比下退化甚至失效。作者指出"各约束各自成熟，但交集未被解决"。

Method: 在低光前馈pointmap前端上构建SYS单目流水线，引入显式匹配门控（match gate）；针对嵌入式平台设计执行路径：地图、关键帧与后端跑384像素、跟踪跑256像素，并加入两项逐帧位姿求解修正；评测用标定且可位精确再生的噪声阶梯、重标真实暗光曝光数据，以及Boston Dynamics Spot暗室视频阶梯。

Result: 量化了基线失效模式：在最暗9个等级中，DROID-SLAM在全部9个等级都返回完整却无信息量的轨迹，VGGT-SLAM与CUT3R为8个，pi^3为7个，DPV-SLAM为4个。SYS仅返回3条有效跟踪轨迹且无无信息轨迹，误差为无信息上限的24–47%（最强基线56–73%），覆盖率最窄（更保守、不硬编造位姿）。Spot实拍视频86.5%帧全黑时，SYS所有配置在亮段后停止，DROID-SLAM与DPV-SLAM仍为全部1178帧输出位姿。Jetson AGX Orin上：1.28x吞吐@0.964x误差、1.42x@0.68x误差，峰值GPU显存降47%、每个位姿能耗降29%。

Conclusion: 在低光实时单目定位这一"交集"问题上，SYS通过门控式前馈pointmap前端+嵌入式优化路径，实现了比实时SLAM基线更可信的失效行为与更低的有效误差，同时显著降低显存与能耗，但代价是覆盖率更窄（会主动放弃无信息场景）。

Abstract: Robots carrying out tasks in dark environments need to localize from a single RGB camera, in light so low that the per-pixel signal approaches the sensor's own noise, on a power-constrained onboard computer, in real time. Each of these constraints has matured pipelines, but the intersection does not. Offline low-light reconstruction now recovers structure below -4 dB but is far too slow to run in real time, while the real-time monocular systems a robot can actually carry (DROID-SLAM, DPV-SLAM, etc.) degrade or fail when SNR gets low. We measured how they fail: across the nine lowest darkness levels of our scenes, DROID-SLAM returns a full-length trajectory carrying no information about the camera's motion on all nine, VGGT-SLAM and CUT3R on eight, pi^3 on seven, and DPV-SLAM on four. We present SYS, a monocular pipeline built on a low-light feed-forward pointmap front end with an explicit match gate, which returns three tracked trajectories and no uninformative ones, at the lowest error of any method where it tracks (24-47% of the no-information ceiling against 56-73% for the strongest baseline), and at the narrowest coverage. On a real robot video take in which 86.5% of delivered frames are entirely black, every configuration of ours stops after the lit beginning, while DROID-SLAM and DPV-SLAM each emit a pose for all 1178 frames. Our method contribution is an embedded execution path for the Jetson AGX Orin: running the map, keyframes and backend at 384 pixels with tracking at 256, together with two fixes to the per-frame pose solve, is a replicated Pareto improvement, 1.28x throughput at 0.964x error on one scene and 1.42x at 0.68x on a second, with 47% less peak GPU memory and 29% less energy per pose. We evaluate on a calibrated, bit-exact regenerable noise ladder, on relabelled real-world dark exposures, and on a new dark-room video ladder recorded from a Boston Dynamics Spot robot.

</details>


### [96] [MetaPusher: Meta Learning and Planning for Nonprehensile Manipulation of Unseen Objects with Rapid Online Adaption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21122)
*Donghyung Lee,Seyedali Golestaneh,Jaskrit Singh,Zhuoyun Zhong,Athanasios Kapoutsis,Constantinos Chamzas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MetaPusher 提出元学习动力学模型与自适应运动动力学规划器耦合的框架，在无目标物体先验交互情况下实现未见物体的非抓取推动操作，仿真与真机迁移中成功率提升最高 20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未见物体的动力学依赖摩擦、质量分布等无法仅从感知推断的潜在物理属性；跨物体先验估计不确定，且在仿真到真实迁移中可能进一步退化。交互中自适应更新模型能逐步修正估计，但模型更新会使已规划轨迹失效，因此需要快速动力学自适应与可融合模型演化的规划策略。

Method: MetaPusher 框架：元学习的动力学模型在任务执行中通过交互快速自适应；自适应运动动力学规划器通过复用并细化已有搜索树来更新长时域规划。二者耦合使操作与自适应过程无需独立的数据采集阶段。

Result: 在仿真与 sim-to-real 场景对未见物体进行评测，与微调、主动学习方法、基于 MPPI 的控制及强化学习策略对比。预测误差更低，任务成功率最高提升 20%。

Conclusion: 元学习自适应动力学与增量式运动动力学规划的结合，可在无物体特定先验交互下高效完成未见物体的非抓取操作，并有效缓解 sim-to-real 迁移中的动力学不确定性。

Abstract: Manipulating previously unseen objects remains challenging, as their dynamics depend on latent physical properties, such as friction and mass distribution, that cannot be inferred from perception alone. Prior experience across objects can provide an initial estimate of unseen object dynamics, but this estimate remains uncertain and can degrade further during sim-to-real transfer. Adapting the dynamics through interaction can progressively refine the estimation, however, updating the model may invalidate the planned trajectory. Successful and efficient manipulation therefore requires both rapid dynamics adaptation and a planning strategy that can incorporate this evolution. In this work, we introduce MetaPusher, a meta-learning and adaptive planning framework for nonprehensile manipulation of unseen objects without prior object-specific interactions. A meta-learned dynamics model rapidly adapts from interactions during task execution, while an adaptive kinodynamic planner updates long-horizon plans by reusing and refining its existing search tree. This coupling enables manipulation and adaptation without a separate data collection phase. We evaluate MetaPusher on unseen objects in simulation and in sim-to-real scenarios, comparing against fine-tuning and active learning methods, MPPI-based control, and a reinforcement learning policy. It achieves lower prediction error and improves task success rate by up to 20%.

</details>


### [97] [SAGE: Safety-Aligned Gradient Enforcement for Human--Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21130)
*Yisen Li,Hao Zhang,Ruize Geng,Yves Tseng,Ding Zhao,H. Eric Tseng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SAGE 框架，用可微安全过滤内化(SAIL)与团队级 Lyapunov 策略优化(TALO)解决"可解释决策树+CBF 过滤"架构在 MARL 中的两处学习错配，实现多人-机器人协作的安全与可解释兼顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人-机器人协作需同时满足决策可解释/可审计与物理交互安全约束。将可解释决策树策略与 CBF 过滤结合虽有前景，但带来两个学习错配：(1) 安全投影改变了实际作用于环境的动作，导致"提议动作-执行动作"不一致；(2) 耦合的提议图使独立优化的 actor 更新与团队级更新目标不对齐。

Method: SAGE 双组件：(1) SAIL(shiled-annealed internalization layer)采用可微的有限惩罚提议映射，执行阶段仍保留精确 CBF 二次规划，从而保持约束法向敏感性，将反复激活的安全约束内化到策略中；(2) TALO(team-averaged Lyapunov policy optimization)构建团队感知的更新参考，并用 Lyapunov 半空间校正约束各 actor 的独立更新。

Result: 两足人形机器人+人类伙伴的物理实验验证可部署性；九个仿真场景下成功率 71.0%，每千步碰撞 0.5 次。消融：直接 CBF 过滤使碰撞降低 98.5%，但成功率从 67.3% 降至 59.3%；SAIL 使提议违反降低 48.8%、提议-执行校正降低 85.2%；TALO 使更新一致性差距降低 50.8%。

Conclusion: 将安全约束内化与团队级策略优化统一到同一 MARL 目标中，可缓解安全投影与独立 actor 更新带来的错配，在保持安全性的同时提升任务成功率，并具备真实人形机器人部署能力。

Abstract: Multi-party human-robot collaboration poses a dual challenge: robot decisions should remain interpretable and auditable, while executed actions must satisfy safety constraints during physical interaction. Combining explainable decision-tree policies with control-barrier-function (CBF) filtering provides a promising architecture but creates two learning mismatches in multi-agent reinforcement learning. Safety projection changes the action applied to the environment, while the coupled proposal graph can misalign independently optimized actor updates with a team-level update. We present safety-aligned gradient enforcement (SAGE) to address both mismatches. Its shield-annealed internalization layer (SAIL) uses a differentiable finite-penalty proposal map while retaining the exact CBF quadratic program for execution, preserving constraint-normal sensitivity to internalize repeatedly active safety constraints. Team-averaged Lyapunov policy optimization (TALO) constructs a team-aware update reference and applies a Lyapunov half-space correction to regulate independent actor updates. Physical experiments with two humanoid robots and a human partner demonstrate deployment feasibility. Across nine simulation scenarios, SAGE achieves a 71.0% success rate with 0.5 collision steps per thousand environment steps. Ablations show that direct CBF filtering reduces collision frequency by 98.5% but decreases success from 67.3% to 59.3%. SAIL reduces proposal violation by 48.8% and proposal-execution correction by 85.2%, while TALO reduces the update-consistency gap by 50.8%.

</details>


### [98] [Diverse and Adaptable Arm Coordination for Octopus-Crawling via Diffusion-Based Uncertainty-Aware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21138)
*Seung Hyun Kim,Heng-Sheng Chang,Kimia Kazemi,Prashant Mehta,Mattia Gazzola*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DUO 算法，用扩散模型+不确定性感知的在线黑箱优化，为肌肉驱动的仿真 CyberOctopus 学习无演示的爬行控制器，实现多样协调模式的发现与保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 章鱼爬行体现了软体多臂机器人冗余控制的优势，但如何发现并组织多样的协调模式以适应复杂物理约束仍是难题。作者希望无需演示数据即可学到多样化的运动控制策略。

Method: (i) 对称结构化的策略表示，将径向等价的控制器折叠到规范方向扇区；(ii) 在线黑箱优化算法 DUO，基于扩散策略分布并引入不确定性感知，以发现和保留多样协调模式；(iii) 控制编辑技术，无需重训练即可将现有控制器迁移到新执行器约束。

Result: 在富接触的仿真环境中，仿真章鱼能够应对动态物理约束并完成爬行导航；学习到的协调多样性被证明有助于鲁棒的适应能力，且控制编辑可适配新约束。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将扩散控制应用于软体多臂机器人，表明学习到的协调多样性可将运动冗余转化为软体多臂机器人适应的实用资源。

Abstract: Octopus crawling motivates soft robots that exploit redundancy, yet discovering and organizing diverse coordination modes for adaptation remains challenging. To address this, we introduce a Diffusion-based Uncertainty-aware Optimization (DUO) algorithm that learns demonstration-free crawling controllers for a simulated, muscle-actuated CyberOctopus. This work represents the first application of diffusion-based control to soft multi-arm robots in contact-rich simulations. By embedding a variety of locomotion behaviors within a shared control distribution, this approach enables the simulated octopus to navigate dynamic physical constraints, demonstrating that learned coordination diversity inherently facilitates robust adaptation. The main contributions include: (i) a symmetry-structured policy representation that folds radially equivalent controllers into a canonical directional sector, (ii) an online black-box optimization strategy, the DUO algorithm, that discovers and retains diverse coordination modes, and (iii) a control editing technique that adapts existing controllers to novel actuator constraints without retraining. These results show how learned coordination diversity makes motor abundance a practical resource for adaptation in soft multi-arm robots.

</details>


### [99] [Same World, Different Knowledge: When Isolated Audits Misjudge World-Model Repairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21155)
*Rui Min,Xianyao Li,Fang Xu,Sofiane Lachab,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出"信息接口审计"方法，区分世界模型的保真度缺口与可用性缺口，并在四旋翼MPC仿真中表明修复方案优劣取决于部署时的信息路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在孤立输入故障下有效的修复策略，一旦部署模块共享故障信息就可能失效；需要一种审计框架来刻画世界模型在真实部署信息路径下的脆弱性与修复效果。

Method: 提出信息接口审计：区分保真度缺口（精确输入变为估计值）与可用性缺口（输入缺失）；采用固定权重干预，度量预测误差、输入依赖性与成对闭环收益，并纳入重构引入的依赖。在仿真四旋翼MPC中检验耦合、异号的10%质量/推力标定误差对物理锚定模型的影响，对比不确定性训练与风场重构。

Result: 耦合异号10%标定误差使物理锚定模型成功率从69%降至8%，不确定性训练恢复至65%；风场重构恢复控制收益但继承标定依赖。正标定偏移下，仅重构损坏偏向不确定性训练重构，共享损坏偏向基线。加速度诊断揭示重构偏差与标称模型误差间的补偿，扰动观测器中亦观察到类似现象。

Conclusion: 修复策略的选择取决于部署中实际使用的信息路径，而非孤立故障下的表现；信息接口审计可指导世界模型修复方案的评估与选择。

Abstract: A repair favored under an isolated input fault can be inferior when deployed modules share the faulty information. We introduce an information-interface audit for world models, distinguishing fidelity gaps, where exact inputs become estimates, from availability gaps, where inputs are missing. Fixed-weight interventions measure prediction error, input dependence, and paired closed-loop benefit, including dependencies introduced by reconstruction. In simulated quadrotor model predictive control, coupled, opposite-sign 10% mass/thrust calibration errors reduce a physics-anchored model's success from 69% to 8%; uncertainty training restores 65%. Wind reconstruction recovers control benefit but inherits calibration dependence. For a positive calibration offset, reconstruction-only corruption favors uncertainty-trained reconstruction, whereas shared corruption favors the baseline. Acceleration diagnostics reveal compensation between reconstruction bias and nominal-model error, also observed with a disturbance observer. Repair selection therefore depends on the information paths used in deployment.

</details>


### [100] [MA-LIPP: Cooperative Multi-Agent Load-Aware Informative Path Planning for Heterogeneous Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21167)
*Hojune Kim,Guangyao Shi,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多智能体负载感知信息路径规划（MA-LIPP），通过异步"死投"实现异构机器人间的样本交接，以MIQP精确求解并配合Pairwise LNS启发式，在最多12机器人规模上匹配95.5%最优解并降低后验方差16.1%–19.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单机器人负载感知信息路径规划中，采样与搬运被强耦合：机器人必须亲自运送每个样本，导致频繁返回基地，严重压缩空间覆盖范围。异构多机器人团队可通过分工（高精度采样器采集、高容量搬运器运输）打破该瓶颈，但随之引入"何时、何地、交接什么、交给谁"的协同难题。现有方法缺乏同时处理负载约束、采样顺序依赖与异步交接的规划框架。

Method: 将MA-LIPP形式化为精确的混合整数二次规划（MIQP），使目标为最小化加权后验方差并显式建模载荷累积对移动能耗与信息增益的影响；同时提出可扩展的成对大邻域搜索（Pairwise LNS）启发式，用于大规模实例的近似求解。核心机制为异步"死投"（dead drops）：机器人可在无同步会合要求下放置样本供他者稍后取回。

Result: 在已验证的案例中，Pairwise LNS启发式有95.5%匹配精确最优解；在最多12个机器人的较大规模实例上，相对顺序基线将加权后验方差降低16.1%–19.8%。

Conclusion: MA-LIPP为协同物理采样任务提供了兼顾精确性与可扩展性的框架，证明异步交接机制可有效解耦采样与运输、扩大覆盖并提升信息获取效率；后续可拓展至更复杂的真实野外任务与在线不确定性场景。

Abstract: Field robotics missions often require physical samples to be returned to laboratories for analysis, making path planning inherently load-aware and order-dependent as accumulated samples increase payload and traversal energy costs. In single-robot Load-Aware Informative Path Planning (LIPP), this rigidly couples sensing with hauling: a solitary robot must transport every collected sample, forcing frequent depot returns that severely restrict its spatial coverage. Heterogeneous multi-robot teams can overcome this bottleneck by dividing labor---enabling high-precision samplers to collect while high-capacity carriers handle transport. However, this introduces a complex coordination challenge regarding when, where, what, and to whom handoffs should occur on top of the LIPP problem. To address this tightly coupled problem, we introduce Multi-Agent LIPP (MA-LIPP), which enables teams to cooperate through asynchronous "dead drops," allowing one robot to deposit samples for another to retrieve later without requiring synchronous rendezvous. We formulate MA-LIPP as an exact Mixed-Integer Quadratic Program (MIQP) alongside a scalable Pairwise Large-Neighborhood Search (LNS) heuristic for complex real-world applications. The heuristic matches exact optima in $95.5\%$ of certified cases and reduces weighted posterior variance by $16.1$--$19.8\%$ relative to a sequential baseline on larger instances of up to 12 robots, providing a robust framework for cooperative physical-sampling missions.

</details>


### [101] [OpenRoIS: A Community-Driven Open-Source Middleware Implementing the Robotic Interaction Service (RoIS) Framework for Physical Robots and Virtual Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21178)
*Sebastian Carrera Villalobos,Christopher Nolan Arellano,Arne Hitzmann,Edilson Morais Brito,Akira Utsumi,Yukiko Horikawa,Takahiro Miyashita,Lotfi El Hafi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenRoIS：RoIS 2.0 标准的开源中间件实现，提供递归引擎架构、五方法内部组件契约、JSON-RPC 2.0/WebSocket 映射、单源类型流水线生成三套语言栈及 TS/C#/Python SDK，使应用可跨硬件访问实体机器人与虚拟代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HRI 服务应用通常绑定特定硬件接口，换硬件即需重写；OMG 的 RoIS Framework 2.0 虽给出平台无关抽象模型（标准化接口+硬件无关符号消息），但仅有规范、缺少可维护实现、SDK 与适配器，难以落地。

Method: 以社区驱动开源方式实现 RoIS 2.0：单一引擎类同时承担主/子 HRI Engine 角色的递归引擎架构；内部五方法组件契约与对外五个 RoIS 接口分离；接口映射到 WebSocket 上的 JSON-RPC 2.0；单源类型流水线生成三套一致语言栈；提供含 Web/Unity 的 TypeScript、C# 客户端 SDK 与含 ROS 2 的 Python 适配器 SDK。

Result: 通过统一 RoIS 接口，服务应用可跨互联网寻址物理机器人与虚拟代理；全部源码、接口类型与文档以 Apache-2.0 开源发布。

Conclusion: 范式无关、开放开发的实现是把标准从规范推向实践的关键；OpenRoIS 为 HRI 服务互操作提供了可直接复用的中间件与多语言 SDK 基础。

Abstract: Service applications for human-robot interaction are commonly written against the hardware-specific interfaces of one platform, so a change of hardware forces a rewrite of the application. The Robotic Interaction Service (RoIS) Framework 2.0, standardized by the Object Management Group (OMG), addresses this fragmentation by defining a platform-independent model in which Service Applications interact with Human-Robot Interaction (HRI) Engines through standardized interfaces and hardware-independent symbolic messages. A specification alone, however, does not provide the maintained implementation, Software Development Kits (SDKs), and adapters needed for practical adoption. This paper presents OpenRoIS, a community-driven open-source middleware providing a concrete implementation of the RoIS Framework 2.0. It takes the position that an openly developed, paradigm-neutral implementation is what carries the standard from specification to practice. OpenRoIS contributes a recursive engine architecture in which a single engine class realizes the main and sub HRI Engine roles, an internal five-method component contract distinct from the five external RoIS interfaces, a mapping of those interfaces onto JSON-RPC 2.0 over WebSocket, a single-source-of-truth type pipeline that generates three consistent language stacks, TypeScript and C# client SDKs that include web and Unity support, and a Python adapter SDK that includes ROS 2 support. Through the common RoIS interfaces, a Service Application can address physical robots and virtual agents over the internet. All source code, interface types, and documentation are released under the Apache-2.0 license and openly developed at this https URL .

</details>


### [102] [When to Waddle: A Comparative Study of Bipedal Torso-Stabilization on Low-Friction Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21185)
*Naomi Oke,Ben Gu,George Ortiz,Stacy Ashlyn,Cordelia Pride,Sarah Bergbreiter,Aaron M. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 受企鹅摇摆步态启发，研究横向躯干运动与质心位置如何影响双足机器人在低摩擦表面的运动表现，并在仿真与硬件上验证其可调节前进速度、能耗与滑移相关足部运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低摩擦表面限制了迈步时可用接触力，是双足运动的挑战；企鹅式摇摆可能通过横向躯干运动与质心布置改善低摩擦下的运动能力，其机理与适用条件尚不清楚。

Method: 使用五驱动双足平台，对比"直立步态"与"躯干越过支撑腿"（企鹅式）两种策略，并改变多个质心位置。在MuJoCo中扫描正弦腿/髋驱动参数，覆盖四个摩擦系数0.1/0.3/0.5/0.7；并开展硬件实验，测试两种质心比例、摩擦系数0.12与0.45下的前进速度、能耗与侧向足部运动。

Result: 仿真中低摩擦下躯干越过支撑腿策略成功率更高、前进速度更快，高质心配置速度最高。硬件上μ=0.12时该策略提升速度并降低运输成本，高质心同时改善两项指标，且侧向足部运动小；μ=0.45时趋势反转，低质心更快更节能，步态策略对速度影响小但仍改变侧向足部运动。

Conclusion: 横向躯干运动与质心布置的效益依赖可用摩擦：低摩擦适合企鹅式高质心步态，高摩擦则低质心更优；由此可在硬件上通过企鹅式躯干运动调节前进速度、能耗与滑移相关足部运动。

Abstract: Low-friction surfaces challenge bipedal locomotion by limiting the contact forces available during stepping. Inspired by penguin waddling, we investigate how lateral torso motion and center of mass (COM) placement affect locomotion as surface friction changes. Using a five-actuator biped, we compare an upright-gait strategy with a penguin-inspired torso-over-stance-leg strategy across multiple COM placements in simulation and hardware. In the 3-D simulator MuJoCo, we sweep through sinusoidal leg and hip actuation parameters across four friction coefficients mu = 0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7. In simulation, torso-over-stance-leg motion produces more successful controllers and higher forward speeds at low friction, with the highest speed occurring for the high-COM configuration. Hardware experiments show the same low-friction speed trend: at mu=0.12, torso-over-stance-leg motion increases forward speed and reduces cost of transport at both tested COM ratios, and the higher COM also improves both measures. The high-COM penguin configuration is the fastest and most energy efficient while maintaining low sideways foot motion. At mu=0.45, the COM trend reverses: the lower-COM configurations are faster and more energy efficient, while gait strategy has little effect on forward speed but still changes sideways foot motion. These results show that the effects of lateral torso motion and COM placement depend on the available friction, and that forward speed, energy use, and slip-related foot motion can be modulated with a penguin-inspired torso motion on hardware.

</details>


### [103] [Robust Structureless Monocular Visual Inertial Initialization Exploiting Line Features and Vanishing Points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21186)
*Junwan Choi,Woongrae Jo,Dong-Uk Seo,Jinwoo Jeon,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SLIM-init：一种无结构单目VIO初始化器，直接利用2D线特征几何约束，无需显式3D重建，在退化运动下提升初始化精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VIO初始化依赖充分激励运动，退化运动（低视差、平移主导）下病态；现有方法或需限制性激励运动，或依赖昂贵的3D结构重建，难以高效实用部署。

Method: 无结构（structureless）单目VIO初始化器。利用线特征推导的消失点（VP）作为平移不变的朝向线索，提供纯旋转约束；引入线对极残差约束平移；引入线法向投影残差改善线性对齐条件数。

Result: 在公开基准与自建退化运动序列上，初始化精度与鲁棒性优于现有SOTA初始化方法，代码开源。

Conclusion: 线特征几何约束可在无需3D重建前提下缓解VIO退化运动初始化问题，具备实用部署潜力。

Abstract: Accurate initialization is essential for reliable visual-inertial odometry (VIO), but it is often ill-conditioned under degenerate motions. Existing methods typically require restrictive excitation motions to ensure sufficient observability or rely on computationally expensive 3D structure reconstruction, limiting efficient and practical deployment. To address these limitations, we propose SLIM-init, a structureless monocular VIO initializer that directly exploits geometric constraints from tracked 2D line features without explicit 3D landmark reconstruction. Specifically, SLIM-init leverages line-derived vanishing points (VPs) as translation-invariant orientation cues to provide robust rotation-only constraints under degenerate scenarios such as low-parallax or translation-dominant motions. It further incorporates a line epipolar residual to constrain translation and a line-normal projection residual to improve the conditioning of linear alignment, enhancing the accuracy and robustness of initial state estimation. Extensive experiments on a public benchmark and challenging custom degenerate-motion sequences demonstrate improved accuracy and robustness over state-of-the-art initialization methods. The source code is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [104] [Stochastic Neural Signed Swept Volume for Real-time Chance-Constrained Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21211)
*Qingyi Chen,Kevin Zhang,Lucas Chen,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将机器人扫掠体积学习为概率性符号距离场（SDF），量化认知不确定性并融入机会约束轨迹优化，实现考虑传感器噪声的无碰撞运动规划，并在仿真与真机高维操作任务中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运动规划在离散状态上对单一确定性环境模型做碰撞检测，安全性与计算效率之间存在权衡；连续碰撞检测（扫掠体积近似）计算昂贵或过于保守；数据驱动方法虽可学习扫掠体积，但神经模型近似误差大，通常只能作为下游碰撞检测器的粗过滤。需要一种能显式建模不确定性、可量化认知不确定性并融合感知噪声的碰撞模型。

Method: 将扫掠体积表示为概率性符号距离场（probabilistic SDF field），在给定观测下输出带认知不确定性的距离分布；利用该概率场建模感知噪声，并将其纳入机会约束（chance-constrained）轨迹优化框架，使规划在满足碰撞概率约束下进行。

Result: 在存在显著传感器噪声的高维操作问题上验证了方法有效性，涵盖仿真与真实硬件实验，表明概率扫掠体积可用于机会约束轨迹优化并提升噪声环境下的规划安全性。

Conclusion: 学习概率性扫掠体积SDF可作为可量化不确定性的连续碰撞模型，替代确定性单模型假设，并与机会约束优化结合，在真实高维操作任务中具备可行性。

Abstract: Collision-free motion planning requires reliable collision models from sensed environments and validation of states along a continuous trajectory. To make this tractable, most planners check for collision at discrete states along continuous trajectories against a single determinized model of the environment, introducing a trade-off between safety and computational efficiency. While continuous collision checking approaches that approximate the swept volume of the robot exist, they are computationally expensive or overly conservative.
