Abstract: Fine-grained object (FO) manipulation requires robots to distinguish a specified FO from visually similar objects and execute actions reliably despite scene distractors. However, scene-level visual conditioning lacks explicit object selection, while category-level guidance cannot reliably distinguish FOs within the same category. We present a SAM3-guided visuomotor framework that addresses these challenges through persistent object memory and focused visual conditioning. First, we introduce FO Memory-driven SAM3 (FOM-SAM3), which learns reusable FO memory tokens from limited multi-view registration images while keeping SAM3 fully frozen. Through one-vs-rest learning, these tokens encode persistent memories for localizing target FOs and rejecting similar alternatives, which can be stored in a memory bank. Second, we propose Focused Spatial-Appearance Encoding (FSAE), which combines in-FO local appearance features with explicit bounding-box coordinates to condition action policies including Diffusion Policy (DP) and Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT). The effectiveness of the proposed FOM-SAM3 was validated on the FO-30 dataset comprising 30 physical objects across four coarse categories. Across three real-robot FO manipulation tasks, our FOM-SAM3-guided policies demonstrated robustness against distractors, discrimination ability among similar FOs, and extendibility to new FOs.

</details>


### [140] [SynthDemo-RL: Breaking the Zero-Reward Barrier in VLA Adaptation with LLM-Guided Synthetic Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21650)
*Hiroaki Kingetsu,Hiroaki Kurihara,Kaoru Yokoo,Kenji Fukumizu,Manohar Kaul*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SynthDemo-RL教师-学生框架，用仿真特权状态自动合成成功轨迹替代人工示教，蒸馏出VLA学生策略后用稀疏二值奖励PPO精调，在LIBERO-PRO上将27个0%成功率任务全部救回，平均成功率97.8%/97.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA微调依赖人工遥操作示教，成本高；而稀疏二值奖励的强化学习在成功轨迹极少时存在严重探索难题。现有基准（如LIBERO-PRO）中大量任务在无示教条件下成功率为0%，需要一种无需新增人工示教即可获得成功轨迹的方案。

Method: 提出SynthDemo-RL教师-学生框架：1）自动化教师利用仿真器特权状态（privileged state）生成成功操作轨迹；2）用SFT将轨迹蒸馏到VLA学生策略；3）用二值任务成功奖励的PPO精调学生。引入reward coverage（在固定评测协议下至少观测到一次成功的任务占比）作为平均成功率的补充指标。每任务仅需50条合成轨迹。

Result: 在无示教的LIBERO-PRO上，pi_0.5策略有27/57任务成功率为0%；同算力同PPO配方直接RL仅救回10个、17个仍为0%；SynthDemo-RL全部救回27个，Position与Task轴平均成功率分别达97.8%和97.1%。标准LIBERO上达96.0%，与使用50条人工示教的pi_0.5仅差1.7个点。RoboTwin 2.0上验证了流程有效性，并确认MuJoCo孪生中训练的策略轨迹可开环迁移执行于物理机器人。

Conclusion: 仿真特权状态合成轨迹+蒸馏+稀疏奖励RL的组合可在零新增人工示教下解决VLA在困难任务上的零成功率问题，显著优于直接RL，且具备跨基准与sim-to-real开环执行的可行性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models commonly relies on human teleoperation demonstrations, while reinforcement learning (RL) with sparse binary rewards faces an exploration challenge when successful trajectories are rarely sampled. We propose SynthDemo-RL, a teacher-student framework in which an automated teacher converts simulator-privileged state into successful manipulation trajectories, a VLA student is distilled from them by supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and PPO with binary task-success rewards refines the student. We study reward coverage, the fraction of tasks for which at least one success is observed under the fixed evaluation protocol, as a complement to the average success rate. On LIBERO-PRO, a public benchmark of perturbed LIBERO tasks for which no demonstrations exist, 27 of 57 scored tasks are at exactly 0% success for a pi_0.5 policy fine-tuned on the original LIBERO tasks. Direct PPO from this policy, under the same PPO recipe and the same RL compute as SynthDemo-RL's refinement stage, rescues 10 of these 27 tasks and leaves 17 at 0%. SynthDemo-RL, with 50 synthesized trajectories per task and no new human demonstrations, rescues all 27 and reaches average success rates of 97.8% and 97.1% on the Position and Task axes of LIBERO-PRO, respectively. On standard LIBERO, the same pipeline reaches 96.0% with no human demonstrations, within 1.7 points of pi_0.5 trained on 50 human demonstrations per task. We further validate the pipeline on RoboTwin 2.0 and verify that trajectories from a policy trained in a MuJoCo twin execute open-loop on a physical robot.

</details>


### [141] [Outcome-Conditioned End-Effector Geometry Across Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21659)
*Xingyu Lin,Zhuang Li,Zhongrun Wu,Shouquan Zhou,Dehui Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文通过15,000次LIBERO闭环rollout，系统比较四个VLA策略的末端执行器几何轨迹，发现"双成功"策略对的动态时间规整距离显著小于"一成功一失败"对，且该方向性在任务、策略对和多种表示下稳健，但数值倍数随表示变化；低跨策略距离并不意味着可互换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略通过不同动作接口解决同一操作任务，但任务成功率本身无法说明其物理执行是否一致。作者希望回答：不同策略在同一任务上的实际运动几何是否趋同，以及这种一致性在何种意义上成立或不成立。

Method: 在LIBERO环境中执行15,000次闭环rollout，覆盖四个VLA策略；构建3,600个配置匹配（故彼此非独立）的策略对；以归一化DTW距离度量末端执行器轨迹差异；跨任务、策略对及九种采样/带限表示验证顺序稳健性；并做72动作窗口、端点与时长调整、以及复合视觉扰动下的对照分析。

Result: 双成功策略对的中位归一化DTW距离为0.0120 m，而仅一方成功时为0.0380 m；该排序在每项任务、每个策略对和九种表示下均成立，但比例随表示变化数倍，故只报告方向而非固定倍数；双失败对分离更大但支撑样本薄弱，仅作探索性结论；匹配基线仍揭示可测量的异质残差策略差异；成功执行与同任务示范之间的距离，约等于示范彼此之间的距离。

Conclusion: 跨策略末端几何的一致性与任务成功存在系统性关联，但具有表示依赖性，且低距离不构成可互换性证据；在复合视觉压力下策略排序与配对组成同时变化，说明结论受感知条件调节。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies solve the same manipulation task through different action interfaces, but task success alone does not establish whether their physical executions agree. We study cross-policy end-effector geometry in 15,000 closed-loop LIBERO rollouts from four policies. The primary clean-condition analysis forms 3,600 configuration-matched, and therefore dependent, policy pairs. Both-success pairs have a median normalized dynamic time warping distance of 0.0120 m versus 0.0380 m when exactly one policy succeeds. This ordering holds in every task, every policy pair, and nine sampling and band-limited representations; however, the ratio varies severalfold across representations, so we report the direction rather than a fixed multiple. Both-failure pairs are more separated again but rest on thin, uneven support, so we report them as exploratory. Within successful executions, partner replacements separate more across tasks than across initial states. A matched baseline still reveals measurable, heterogeneous residual policy differences, so a low cross-policy distance does not imply interchangeability. Successful executions sit about as far from same-task demonstrations as those demonstrations sit from each other, compatible with task-associated geometry without separating training-data overlap from task constraints. A common 72-action window preserves the ordering but reduces its magnitude; endpoint and duration adjustment likewise leaves a positive mixed-outcome coefficient relative to both-success pairs, though its magnitude is specification-dependent. Under composite visual stress, policy rankings and pair composition change together.

</details>


### [142] [RAYA: Learning Where and When to Intervene for Robot Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21690)
*Ishaan Mahajan,Charles Chen,Frederike Dümbgen,Brian Plancher*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAYA：把可学习有限时域"可恢复性裕度"嵌入硬约束最优控制器，并用有界学习调度器动态调整任务权重，实现"预判失败即可防失败"；仿真7200回合跨无人机/自动驾驶基准提升存活率并零样本迁移，35g Crazyflie实机在有风+气动失配或未建模40%电机指令损失下完成10/10六周期任务，三类基线全部失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人能预测失败却未必能阻止失败：安全机制反应时，名义规划已耗尽了恢复所需的控制权限，而固定任务优先级又阻塞了剩余响应空间。作者的关键洞见是两点都在控制器内部决定——可恢复性应在动作选择时参与决策而非事后否决，任务目标应随可恢复性收缩而自适应调整。

Method: RAYA是一个混合"学习+解析"框架：在学习到的有限时域可恢复性裕度（finite-horizon recoverability margin）被置入带硬约束的最优控制器内部，使可恢复性直接参与动作选取；同时配对一个有界的学习型调度器（bounded learned scheduler），在需要时平滑地移动任务权重以支援恢复。学习组件与硬约束解析控制互补，保证安全约束不被学习部分破坏。

Result: 每个控制器跨四旋翼与自动驾驶两类基准共7200回合仿真：RAYA不仅提升存活率，而且学习组件零样本迁移到未见轨迹、扰动、被控对象偏移与摩擦布局。嵌入式实现部署于35g Crazyflie四旋翼机载：40次组合硬件飞行（有风，叠加气动失配或未建模的40%电机指令损失）中，三个基线全部失败，RAYA完成10/10六周期任务。

Conclusion: 可恢复性必须内生于控制决策而非事后过滤，任务优先级需随裕度变化而自适应；该混合框架在仿真与真实机载飞行中同时获得生存率提升、零样本泛化与抗风抗执行器失效的鲁棒性，表明"可恢复性感知控制"是可落地的机器人安全范式。

Abstract: A robot can predict failure and still be unable to prevent it. By the time a safety mechanism reacts, the nominal plan may already have spent the control authority that recovery requires, and fixed task priorities may block whatever response remains. Our key insight is that both aspects are decided inside the controller. Recoverability must inform actions while they are chosen rather than veto them afterward, and task objectives must be adapted as recoverability shrinks. Building on this, we present RAYA, a hybrid learned-analytic framework that places a learned finite-horizon recoverability margin inside an optimal controller with hard constraints and pairs it with a bounded learned scheduler that shifts task weights to facilitate recovery. Across 7,200 simulation episodes per controller spanning quadrotor and autonomous-vehicle benchmarks, RAYA not only improves survival rates, but also transfers the learned components zero-shot to unseen trajectories, disturbances, plant shifts, and friction layouts. We developed an embedded realization of RAYA and deployed it on-board a 35g Crazyflie quadrotor. Across 40 combined hardware flights under wind with either aerodynamic mismatch or an unmodeled 40% motor-command loss, each of three baselines fails in all trials, while RAYA completes 10/10 six-cycle missions. Project Website: this https URL .

</details>


### [143] [NeuRIO: A Streaming Neural Estimator for Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Multi-Robot Relative Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21707)
*Zhehan Li,Jiadong Lu,Shengwei Ren,Chao Xu,Yanjun Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 NeuRIO，一种仅用机器人间方位、距离与 IMU 的免锚 6-DoF 相对惯性里程计流式神经估计器，基于图注意力+GRU，实现零样本 sim-to-real 与大规模可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人相对定位在无外部锚点、仅依赖机间方位/测距与 IMU 时，传统优化方法随团队规模增长计算代价急剧上升（24 个机器人即超过 20ms），难以支撑大规模、时变观测图的实时估计，且仿真到真机迁移困难。

Method: 将测量规范化到重力对齐坐标系；把机器人建模为图节点、相互观测建模为因子边；用注意力做空间推理、GRU 做时间建模；全网络共享节点级与因子级算子，从而适配不同团队规模与时变观测图。训练于耦合多种运动模式、器件级传感特性及时间持续性传感器退化的仿真器，实现零样本 sim-to-real。

Result: 在 24 段真实序列上达到 14.1 cm 位置 RMSE、3.9° 旋转 RMSE；仿真中 400 个机器人时单次更新仍低于 20ms，而优化方法在 24 个机器人时已超 20ms；仅在有限团队规模训练即可直接迁移到更大团队，无需改动架构或参数。

Conclusion: NeuRIO 以图神经网络流式估计实现了免锚多机器人相对惯性里程计的高精度、零样本 sim-to-real 与近常数级计算可扩展性，为大规模集群相对状态估计提供可行方案。

Abstract: We present NeuRIO, a streaming neural estimator for anchor-free 6-DoF relative inertial odometry using only identified inter-robot bearings, ranges, and IMU measurements. NeuRIO canonicalizes measurements into gravity-aligned coordinates, represents robots as nodes and mutual observations as factors, and uses attention for spatial reasoning and GRUs for temporal modeling. As a graph network, NeuRIO applies shared node-wise and factor-wise operators throughout the network, enabling it to handle different team sizes and time-varying observation graphs. NeuRIO is trained on a simulator that couples various motion patterns, device-level sensor characteristics, and diverse, realistic modeled, and temporally persistent sensor corruptions. In this way, NeuRIO achieves zero-shot sim-to-real transfer. Across $24$ real-world sequences, NeuRIO achieves $14.1\,\mathrm{cm}$ position RMSE and $3.9^\circ$ rotation RMSE. More importantly, NeuRIO demonstrates strong computational scalability, maintaining an update cost below $20\,\mathrm{ms}$ with up to $400$ robots in simulation, while optimization-based methods exceed $20\,\mathrm{ms}$ at only $24$ robots. Moreover, even trained on limited team sizes, NeuRIO transfers directly to unseen larger teams without architectural or parameter changes.

</details>


### [144] [AgenticSwarm: Semantic Perception and Adaptive Task Allocation for Heterogeneous Multi-UAV Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21716)
*Muhammad Ahsan Mustafa,Yasheerah Yaqoot,Faryal Batool,Roohan Ahmed Khan,Valerii Serpiva,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AgenticSwarm 智能体框架，将语义感知（SAM3 流水线）与约束式任务分配结合，实现异构多无人机在复杂环境中的自适应任务规划与残余任务重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机任务在复杂环境下需同时理解场景与人类操作者意图，并在任务条件变化（无人机故障、电池衰减、任务修改）时保持可行的任务分配，现有方法难以将语义推理与约束分配、动态重构统一起来。

Method: 用一个智能体解析航空影像与自然语言指令，构建接地（grounded）任务表征，把感知对象/区域与任务需求、能力约束、任务依赖关联；该表征增强约束式任务分配，分配前纳入避障路径可行性、能耗与受保护返航约束；执行中发生故障、电量下降或任务变更时，基于当前系统状态进行残余任务重构，同时保留已完成工作与侦察进度。采用 SAM3 感知流水线。

Result: 在 5 个 Gazebo 环境和 1 个室内真实测试环境中评估。相比 Grounding DINO+SAM2.1 基线，SAM3 流水线的类别感知召回提升 25.2 pp，语义标签准确率提升 29.5 pp。消融残余任务重规划后，平均重复工作量从 0% 升至 61.7%，事件后恢复时间增加 58.6%。

Conclusion: AgenticSwarm 能够将语义推理、约束分配与自适应多无人机任务执行连接起来，残余任务重规划对降低重复工作与加快恢复具有显著贡献。

Abstract: Multi UAV missions in complex environments require the system to understand both the surrounding scene and the intent of a human operator while maintaining feasible task allocation as mission conditions change. This paper presents AgenticSwarm, an agentic framework for semantic perception and adaptive task allocation in heterogeneous multi UAV missions. An agent interprets aerial imagery and natural language instructions to construct a grounded mission representation that links perceived objects and regions with task requirements, capability constraints, and mission dependencies. This information augments a constrained task allocation process in which obstacle aware path feasibility, energy consumption, and protected return home requirements are incorporated before assignment. During execution, changes such as UAV failure, battery degradation, or task modification trigger residual mission reconstruction from the current system state, while completed work and reconnaissance progress are retained. AgenticSwarm is evaluated across five diverse Gazebo environments and an indoor real test environment, demonstrating its ability to connect semantic reasoning with constrained allocation and adaptive multi UAV mission execution. Compared with a Grounding DINO+SAM~2.1 perception baseline, the SAM3-based pipeline improves class-aware recall by 25.2 percentage points (pp) and semantic label accuracy by 29.5 pp. Ablating residual mission replanning increases mean repeated work from 0% to 61.7% and post-event recovery time by 58.6%, highlighting the contribution of adaptive replanning to mission execution.

</details>


### [145] [A Novel Path-Tracking Algorithm for Automated Tractor-Trailer Forward and Backward Maneuvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21718)
*Alexandre Lombard,Florent Perronnet,Nicolas Gaud,Abdeljalil Abbas-Turki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向单挂车铰接式车辆的几何路径跟踪算法，通过在挂车层级施加横向控制律并对牵引车转向角进行短时域预测修正，实现前进与倒车工况下稳定的路径跟踪，无需逐车标定或训练，并在高保真物理仿真中匹配或超越经典与前沿方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全自动牵引车-挂车系统在物流、农业与工业场景中日益普及，但铰接结构引入额外非线性，使横向与纵向控制显著复杂化，倒车尤为困难。现有方法多依赖逐车标定或学习式训练，泛化性与部署成本受限，因此需要一种无需标定、对扰动鲁棒的路径跟踪方案。

Method: 采用几何建模思路：在挂车层级设计横向控制律以直接约束挂车对目标路径的偏差，同时对牵引车转向角施加短时域预测性调整，通过预测未来若干步的铰接姿态变化来补偿非线性耦合。该方法为解析式几何控制，不含学习模块，也无需车辆参数标定，可统一处理前进与倒车。

Result: 在高保真物理仿真器中的对比研究显示，该控制器在跟踪精度、稳定性以及对扰动的鲁棒性方面达到或优于经典控制方法与当前先进方法；正反向运动均能收敛至期望路径。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，基于几何的挂车级横向控制结合短时域转向预测，可在不依赖标定与训练的前提下有效解决铰接车辆的路径跟踪难题，为自动牵引车-挂车的实际部署提供了一种简洁且具鲁棒性的控制方案；当前验证限于仿真，实车验证与复杂地形、多挂车情形仍待探索。

Abstract: Fully autonomous tractor--trailer systems are increasingly deployed in logistics, agriculture, and industrial environments, where precise and robust path-tracking capabilities are essential. However, the articulation between the tractor and the trailer introduces additional nonlinearities and significantly complicates lateral and longitudinal control, particularly during reversing maneuvers. This paper introduces a novel path-tracking algorithm specifically designed for articulated vehicles with a single trailer. The proposed method combines a lateral control law applied at the trailer level with a short-horizon predictive adjustment of the tractor steering angle, ensuring stable convergence toward the desired path in both forward and backward motion. The approach is geometry-based and requires no per-vehicle calibration or training. Simulation studies in a high-fidelity physics simulator demonstrate the ability of the controller to match or outperform classical and state-of-the-art methods in terms of accuracy, stability, and robustness to disturbances.

</details>


### [146] [ZeroTouch: Tactile-Supervised Visual Contact Estimation for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21726)
*Dmitriy Kosenkov,Daniia Zinniatullina,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Iana Zhura,Mikhail Derevianchenko,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ZeroTouch 提出一种触觉监督框架，仅用腕部 RGB、夹爪状态和局部重力方向，即可预测密集接触形变、六轴力/力矩及抓取相关的目标压缩量，部署时无需触觉硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉传感器能直接测量物理交互，但部署需要专用硬件；本文希望在不依赖触觉硬件的前提下获得触觉式的交互估计，以提升抓取压缩目标的可靠性。

Method: 将触觉测量作为训练期的特权监督信号，输入为腕部 RGB 观测、夹爪状态和局部重力方向，输出为密集接触形变、瞬时六轴 wrench 以及抓取相关的期望压缩目标；触觉仅在训练时使用，部署阶段不需要。

Result: 完整架构将法向力 MAE 从仅状态基线的 2.017 N 降至 0.531 N；物理实验中每条件 20 次试验，未见物体成功率达 95%，已见物体/未见抓取为 80%，内容/负载偏移下为 90%；同协议下 OpenVLA 为 25%/40%/55%，SmolVLA 为 10%/25%/35%。

Conclusion: 触觉监督可作为特权信息蒸馏到视觉-本体感知策略中，在部署时无需触觉硬件即可实现可靠的抓取力/压缩控制，并在多种泛化条件下显著优于 VLA 基线。

Abstract: Reliable robotic grasping benefits from estimating the evolving physical interaction and selecting a grasp-dependent compression target. Tactile sensors provide direct interaction measurements but require dedicated hardware at deployment. We introduce ZeroTouch, a tactile-supervised framework that predicts dense contact deformation, the instantaneous six-axis wrench, and a grasp-dependent desired compression target from wrist RGB observations, gripper state, and local gravity direction. Tactile measurements are used only as privileged supervision during training and are not required at deployment. On the full validation set, the complete architecture reduces normal-force MAE from 2.017 N for a state-only baseline to 0.531 N. In physical evaluation with 20 trials per condition, ZeroTouch achieves 95% success on an unseen object, 80% in a seen-object/unseen-grasp condition, and 90% under a content/load shift. Under the same evaluation protocol, OpenVLA achieves 25%, 40%, and 55%, while SmolVLA achieves 10%, 25%, and 35%, respectively.

</details>


### [147] [Visual Proactivity: Enhancing Human-Robot Collaboration Through Intent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21729)
*Valerio Bo,Edison Bejarano,Anaís Garrell,Alberto Sanfeliu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"视觉主动性"（visual proactivity）概念，让机器人通过视觉反馈主动传达自身意图，在人人-机器人交接场景的用户研究中验证了反应型、预期型与主动型行为的人类感知差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人从重复任务走向人机协作，理解人类意图固然重要，但既有研究较少关注机器人如何"反向"沟通自身意图，导致透明度与协作流畅性受限。

Method: 提出视觉主动性方法，使机器人通过视觉反馈传达其意图；在人到机器人（human-to-robot）物体交接场景中设计并实现反应型（reactive）、预期型（anticipatory）、主动型（proactive）三类机器人行为，并通过用户研究评估人的感知。

Result: 用户研究证实被试能区分三类行为，且有效的视觉主动性带来更好的人机对齐与协调，提升交互透明度与流畅度。

Conclusion: 视觉主动性是一种简单而有效的机器人意图沟通机制，为更直观的人机协作提供了路径；未来可拓展到更复杂任务与多模态意图表达。

Abstract: As robots transition from performing repetitive tasks to collaborating with humans, understanding human intent becomes crucial to effective interaction. Anticipation enables robots to predict human actions, while proactivity allows them to take initiative and guide human behavior toward optimal outcomes. Although research has largely focused on how robots infer and respond to human intentions, less attention has been paid to how robots communicate their own intent. This paper introduces visual proactivity, a novel, simple yet effective approach that enables robots to communicate their intentions through visual feedback, influencing human behavior and enhancing transparency and fluency. We develop and evaluate proactive robotic behaviors in a human-to-robot handover scenario, where a user study validates human perception of reactive, anticipatory, and proactive behaviors. The results demonstrate that effective visual proactivity fosters better alignment and coordination, paving the way for more intuitive human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [148] [When Should Robots Intervene? Balancing Engagement and Intrusiveness in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21734)
*Lavinia Hriscu,Valerio Bo,Alberto Sanfeliu,Anaís Garrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过32人模拟医院环境用户研究，对比"持续寻求互动"与"情境感知选择性介入"两种机器人干预策略，借助身体朝向与注意力等多模态线索驱动机器人行为，发现高互动频率未必带来更好参与度，感知支持与侵入性之间存在系统性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务导向场景下的HRI需要在用户参与度与社交可接受的机器人侵入性之间取得平衡，但缺乏关于不同干预策略如何影响用户体验、互动动态与支持感的实证证据。

Method: 提出并对比两种策略：(1) 持续寻求参与的机器人策略；(2) 基于用户状态与任务情境选择性介入的情境感知策略。两者均以身体朝向、注意力信号等多模态行为线索指导机器人动作。在模拟医院环境中开展32名被试的用户研究进行评测。

Result: 更高互动频率并不必然带来更好参与度；观察到感知支持与侵入性之间的系统性权衡，且该权衡受物理距离、用户投入努力等因素影响。

Conclusion: 有效的HRI参与度依赖自适应、情境敏感的干预策略，而非单纯的互动频率提升；为社交性干预设计提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Designing effective Human-Robot Interaction in task-oriented settings requires carefully balancing user engagement with socially acceptable levels of robot intrusiveness. In this paper, we examine how different robot intervention strategies shape user experience, interaction dynamics, perceived intrusiveness, and sense of support. We compare two approaches: a continuous engagement-seeking robot strategy, and a context-aware strategy that selectively intervenes based on the user's state and task context. Both approaches rely on multimodal behavioral cues, including body orientation and attentional signals, to guide robot actions. We evaluate these strategies in a user study with 32 participants performing a task in a simulated hospital environment. Our findings show that higher interaction frequency does not necessarily lead to better engagement. Instead, we observe a systematic trade-off between perceived support and intrusiveness, influenced by factors such as physical proximity and user effort. These results provide empirical evidence that effective engagement in HRI depends on adaptive, context-sensitive intervention policies.

</details>


### [149] [Sandwich-Residuals: Parameter-Efficient Test-time Adaptation of World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21740)
*Krishnam Soni,Aditya Sehgal,Vedant Dave,Elmar Rueckert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Sandwich-Residuals：冻结预训练世界模型，仅在预测器周围学习小型残差修正，用自监督预测误差在线优化，在 AdaJEPA 与 DINO-WM 上大幅减少可调参数并提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型通过在学习到的表示空间中预测动作效果来做规划，但在测试条件与训练分布不一致时预测会不可靠。现有测试时自适应方法通常更新预训练模型的大量参数（百万级），还需人为选择适配的内部组件，代价高且设计繁琐。

Method: 保持预训练世界模型完全冻结，仅在预测器（predictor）外围引入轻量残差修正项（Sandwich-Residuals）；残差以在线方式优化，监督信号为模型自身的自监督预测误差，无需奖励、标签或源域数据。

Result: 在 AdaJEPA 基准的 21 种条件下，成功率约为冻结模型的 1.3 倍，达到最强 AdaJEPA 变体性能的 95%，可调参数减少 97–99%；在复合分布偏移下提升至冻结模型的 1.9 倍成功率，与内部模块适配相当。并在 DINO-WM 的 3D 操作任务上验证了同一适配原则。

Conclusion: 有效的世界模型测试时自适应不必修改其预训练内部权重，仅通过冻结骨干+轻量残差即可获得显著鲁棒性收益，且参数高效、无需额外监督。

Abstract: Latent world models enable planning by predicting the effects of actions in a learned representation space, but their predictions can become unreliable when test-time conditions differ from training. Existing test-time adaptation methods address this by updating parts of the pretrained model, often modifying millions of parameters and requiring a choice of which internal components to adapt. We introduce Sandwich-Residuals, a lightweight alternative that keeps the pretrained world model frozen and learns only small residual corrections around the predictor. The residuals are optimized online using the model's self-supervised prediction error and require no rewards, labels, or source-domain data. Across 21 conditions on the AdaJEPA benchmark, our method achieves $1.3\times$ the success rate of the frozen model while retaining 95% of the performance of the strongest AdaJEPA variant and adapting 97-99% fewer parameters. Under compound shifts, this advantage increases to $1.9\times$ the success rate of the frozen model, while remaining comparable to internal block adaptation. We further demonstrate the same adaptation principle on a DINO-WM model for 3-D manipulation. These results suggest that effective test-time adaptation of world models does not necessarily require modifying their pretrained internal weights.

</details>


### [150] [Understanding Engagement and Intrusiveness in Assistive Human-Robot Interaction Using Individual Traits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21744)
*Valerio Bo,Lavinia Hriscu,Alberto Sanfeliu,Anaís Garrell*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现机器人接近距离对用户参与度的混合效应实际上受干预频率和个体特质（人格、人口学）共同调节：32人被试实验表明总体参与度稳定，但情感反应与入侵感随人格显著变化，且人格对交互动态的影响依赖机器人行为策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于机器人近距学（proxemics）对用户参与度的研究结论相互矛盾（近距增强交互 vs. 造成入侵感），说明单一空间因素解释力不足，需要引入干预频率与个体差异（人格、人口学）作为调节变量。

Method: 被试内/被试间实验，32名参与者与两个任务支持能力相似但干预策略不同的辅助机器人交互；测量参与度、情感反应与感知入侵感，并分析其与人格特质和人口学变量的关系。

Result: 整体参与度在不同条件下保持稳定；情感反应和感知入侵感随人格特质显著变化；人格对交互动态的影响因机器人行为策略不同而不同。

Conclusion: 有效的HRI应纳入个体差异，依据用户独特的情感与行为画像定制机器人行为，以在尊重用户的同時维持参与度。

Abstract: Robot assistance is particularly crucial in unfamiliar tasks, where users must understand task requirements while coordinating with the robot. Previous research offers mixed evidence on the role of robot proxemics in user engagement: some studies suggest closer proximity enhances interaction, while others report it can feel intrusive. In this work, we argue that perceptions of intrusiveness depend not only on proxemics but also on the frequency of robot interventions, and are strongly influenced by individual traits such as personality and demographics. We conducted an experiment with 32 participants who interacted with two assistive robots that provided similar task support but differed in their intervention strategies. Results indicate that overall engagement remains stable across conditions, yet affective responses and perceived intrusiveness vary significantly with personality traits. Moreover, personality shapes interaction dynamics differently depending on the robot's behavior. These findings emphasize that effective human-robot interaction should account for individual differences, tailoring robot behavior to maintain engagement while respecting each user's unique affective and behavioral profile.

</details>


### [151] [ForceTwin: Physics-informed Digital Twins for Robotic Manipulation from Instrumented Human Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21751)
*Tim Engelbracht,René Zurbrügg,Mayank Mittal,Marco Hutter,Marc Pollefeys,Hermann Blum,Zuria Bauer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForceTwin通过手持力觉夹爪在真实交互中辨识铰接物体的物理数字孪生，含惯量、库仑/粘性摩擦及状态相关的机构力神经残差，并用于阻抗控制与全身开门策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接物体的可操作性由惯量、摩擦与弹簧/闭门器等机构决定，但这些属性无法从外观直接观测：视觉相同的门推动所需力可能差异很大。现有数字孪生管线多只恢复运动学，或用视觉-语言先验赋予静态物理参数，导致物理上不合理，且状态相关的机构动力学未被建模，也无法写入标准资产格式。

Method: 使用带力感知的手持夹爪由人探测物体，采集同步的位姿与交互力；据此估计铰接结构，并辨识参数化动力学（惯量、库仑摩擦、粘性阻尼），再以结构化神经残差建模状态相关的机构力；辨识结果作为前馈动力学模型用于阻抗控制，并用于训练全身开门穿越策略。

Result: 惯量参数误差相对VLM先验降低近一半；作为Spot与Franka FR3上阻抗控制的前馈模型，在9组物体-本体配对中目标完成率87%，对比VLM先验60%、仅运动学孪生57%，在强机构导致基线停滞的物体上增益最大；所辨识孪生还用于训练全身开门穿越策略并在真实世界部署。

Conclusion: 以仪表化人类交互为数据源、将参数化动力学与结构化神经残差结合，可得到物理一致的铰接物体数字孪生；该孪生既能提升接触丰富操作的控制器性能，也能支撑真实机器人全身策略学习，说明"力觉交互辨识"是弥补外观到物理鸿沟的有效途径。

Abstract: Manipulating objects requires understanding not only their motion, but also the physical properties that determine it. For articulated objects, these include inertia, friction, and mechanisms such as springs or door closers, whose effects can vary with configuration and velocity. Such properties are not directly observable from appearance: visually identical doors may require very different effort to manipulate. Existing digital-twin pipelines recover primarily kinematics or assign static physical parameters from visual and language priors, which can yield physically implausible estimates. As a result, state-dependent mechanism dynamics remain unidentified and are not represented in standard asset formats. We present ForceTwin, a system for identifying physics-informed digital twins of articulated objects from instrumented human interaction. A person probes an object using a handheld force-sensing gripper, providing synchronized poses and interaction forces from which we estimate the articulation, parametric dynamics including inertia, Coulomb friction, viscous damping, and a structured neural residual capturing state-dependent mechanism forces. ForceTwin nearly halves the inertial-parameter error of a VLM prior. As a feedforward dynamics model for impedance control on a Spot and a Franka FR3, ForceTwin achieves 87% goal completion across nine object-embodiment pairs, compared with 60% using VLM-prior and 57% using kinematics-only twins, with the largest gains on objects whose strong mechanisms cause both baselines to stall. We further use the identified twins to train whole-body door-traversal policies and deploy them in the real world. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [152] [PSR: Predictive Sensorimotor Representation Learning for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21753)
*Shengbao Li,Peng Xu,Chao Tang,Hao Wei,Jiaheng Wang,Hong Yin,Jiangtao Chen,Jinxuan Zhu,Zhong Zhou,Mengfan Wang,Tingguang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出预测性感知运动表征（PSR）框架，通过多模态Transformer联合预测未来交互动力学，学习分层预测表征并注入VLA动作流，在6个真实接触丰富任务上达到91.7%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富操作需要策略在视觉之外推理接触力、机器人构型与交互历史。现有方法多为被动地以力反馈为条件，不主动预测未来接触动力学，因而难以生成高精度动作。

Method: 预训练阶段用多模态Transformer对多模态感知运动信号联合预测未来交互动力学，学得分层预测表征；该表征随后增强策略的动作流（action stream），使策略在多个深度上利用接触相关线索。并将PSR实例化到VLA模型中，得到PSR-VLA。

Result: 在6个真实世界接触丰富操作任务上，PSR-VLA总体成功率91.7%，相较π0.5、ForceVLA-π0.5、ForceVLA2-π0.5分别提升30.0、22.5、19.2个百分点。

Conclusion: 主动预测式分层感知运动表征能有效提升力感知的接触丰富操作性能，为VLA策略注入接触动力学先验是有前景的方向。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires policies to generate precise actions by reasoning over contact forces, robot configurations, and interaction histories beyond visual observations. Existing methods passively condition on force feedback rather than actively predicting future contact dynamics, limiting their ability to generate high-precision actions. To address this problem, we introduce Predictive Sensorimotor Representation (PSR) learning, a framework that learns a hierarchy of predictive representations from multimodal sensorimotor signals and integrates them into the action stream of a visuomotor policy. Specifically, during a pretraining stage, a multimodal Transformer is trained to learn a hierarchy of predictive representations by jointly forecasting future interaction dynamics. The learned hierarchy subsequently augments the action stream, enabling the resulting policy to exploit contact-relevant cues at multiple depths. We further instantiate PSR within a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model, resulting in PSR-VLA, and evaluate it on six real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks. Experimental results show that PSR-VLA achieves 91.7% overall success, improving over $\pi_{0.5}$, ForceVLA-$\pi_{0.5}$, and ForceVLA2-$\pi_{0.5}$ by 30.0, 22.5, and 19.2 percentage points, respectively. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed PSR for force-aware, contact-rich manipulation. Videos of the tasks and stability tests are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [153] [CRISP: Contact-Rich Robotic Simulation Platform with Extensive Geometries and Contact Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21761)
*Somang Lee,Sunkyung Park,Jinhee Yun,Seoki An,Dongjun Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CRISP 是一个面向高精度多接触机器人操作的高保真物理仿真平台，结合表达力强的几何表示、基于优化的精确碰撞检测与增广拉格朗日接触求解器，提升接触密集场景的物理保真度，并与现有先进平台对比验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧公差机器人操作等复杂多接触仿真要求同时具备：富有表达力的几何与接触建模，以及稳健的碰撞检测与接触求解数值方法。现有仿真器要么几何表示与接触建模过于简化，要么数值求解精度/鲁棒性受限，难以准确处理接触密集和尖锐（sharp）交互。

Method: 提出 CRISP 仿真平台：支持多种几何表示，采用基于优化的精确碰撞检测；将接触建模与稳健的增广拉格朗日（augmented Lagrangian）接触求解器结合，从而在复杂几何间一致、高效地检测接触信息，并在不引入有害松弛（relaxation）的前提下求解多接触约束。

Result: 与当前最先进平台进行物理保真度对比验证，并通过复杂机器人操作场景展示其能力；平台已开源。

Conclusion: CRISP 通过几何表示、精确碰撞检测与增广拉格朗日接触求解的统一集成，为接触密集、紧公差机器人操作提供高保真仿真基础，具备开源可复现性。

Abstract: We present CRISP (Contact-RIch Simulation Platform), a high-fidelity physics engine tailored for complex multi-contact simulations such as tight-tolerance robotic manipulation. Achieving high physical fidelity in robotic simulation requires both expressive modeling of geometry and contact interactions, as well as accurate numerical resolution via robust collision detection and contact solvers. However, existing simulators often either rely on limited support for geometric representations and simplified modeling of contact interactions, or employ numerical resolution methods whose accuracy or robustness is inherently constrained. Accordingly, we develop a new simulator that supports diverse geometric representations with accurate optimization-based collision detection, and combines contact modeling with robust augmented Lagrangian-based contact solvers. This integration enables efficient and consistent detection of contact information across complex geometries while accurately resolving multi-contact constraints without problematic relaxations, which is essential for simulating contact-intensive and sharp interactions. We validate the physical fidelity of our simulator against state-of-the-art platforms and further demonstrate its capabilities through complex robotic manipulation scenarios. CRISP is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [154] [Scaling Vision-Language Reward Learning for Robot Manipulation in Parallel Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21767)
*Lobna Joualy,Eric Demeester,Nikolaos Tsiogkas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAPID通过GPU并行rollout、数据感知策略更新、单请求偏好标注、自动奖励稳定化与代表性图像采样，将VLM偏好奖励学习训练时间从9.18小时降至1.15小时（8.0倍加速），API调用从19840降至896（减少95.5%），成功率从86.3%提升至98.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用VLM替代人工标注做偏好奖励学习时，顺序API请求与单环境数据采集导致训练慢、成本高，亟需提升效率与可扩展性。

Method: 提出RAPID系统，耦合四项组件：(1) GPU并行rollout配合数据感知的策略更新；(2) 单请求偏好标注；(3) 自动奖励稳定化；(4) 代表性图像采样。在IsaacLab的五个Franka Panda操作任务上评估，并用Gemma 3 12B与GPT-4.1 mini做离线标注评估。

Result: 两阶段提示下平均运行时间由9.18小时降至3.13小时；全组件开启后训练1.15小时完成，每轮API调用896次而非19840次，聚合最终成功率由86.3%升至98.7%；端到端加速8.0倍，API用量减少95.5%。离线评估显示单请求提示在两种模型上均降低标注延迟与成本。

Conclusion: 并行rollout与自适应更新是训练时间下降的主要来源，单请求标注与图像采样进一步提升效率与成功率，为VLM驱动的奖励学习提供高效可行的工程路径。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) can replace human annotators in preference-based reward learning, but sequential API requests and single-environment data collection make training slow and costly. We present RAPID (Reward learning with Adaptive Parallel Image Diversity), a system that couples GPU-parallel rollout with data-aware policy updates, single-request preference labeling, automatic reward stabilization, and representative image sampling. We evaluate these components on five Franka Panda manipulation tasks in IsaacLab. Parallel rollout and adaptive updates provide the first substantial reduction in training time: under matched two-stage prompting, mean runtime falls from 9.18 to 3.13 hours. With all RAPID components enabled, training completes in 1.15 hours using 896 rather than 19,840 API calls per run, and aggregate final success rises from 86.3\% to 98.7\%. This represents an 8.0$\times$ end-to-end speedup and a 95.5\% reduction in API usage. An offline evaluation with Gemma~3 12B and GPT-4.1 mini demonstrates that single-request prompting reduces labeling latency and cost across both models. Code is available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [155] [TRACE: Coverage Path Planning for Unknown Environments Using Hierarchical Coverage Tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21777)
*Zongyuan Shen,Haodong Liu,Gao Wang,Shancheng Zhao,Dehua Zhou,Yaming Ou,Zhongqiang Ren,Yikui Zhai,C. L. Philip Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出在线覆盖路径规划算法TRACE，基于分层覆盖树表示未覆盖空间连通性，通过增量全局巡游与局部回字形路径结合，实现未知环境下实时全覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知环境下实时覆盖路径规划（CPP）需要应对环境逐步揭示导致的未覆盖空间被分割问题；全局重规划计算代价高，需一种能增量维护覆盖顺序、避免全局重算的方法。

Method: 构建分层覆盖树全局表示未覆盖空间的连通结构；当新障碍或已覆盖栅格使剩余空间碎片化时，递归扩展树节点显式表示各区域；基于更新后的树维护增量全局巡游引导覆盖，仅局部细化受影响部分、保留未变区域的访问顺序；局部规划器生成往复（back-and-forth）覆盖路径，必要时切换为全局巡游感知规划。

Result: 理论分析给出TRACE的计算复杂度、全覆盖性质及增量全局巡游细化的近似界；通过高保真仿真与移动机器人真机实验验证，与六种现有CPP方法对比，在覆盖时间、路径长度、重叠率与转弯次数上均有显著改善。

Conclusion: TRACE以分层树+增量全局巡游的框架有效降低全局重规划开销并保持覆盖进度一致性，兼具理论保证与实验优势，适用于未知环境实时全覆盖任务。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel online coverage path planning (CPP) algorithm, called TRACE, for real-time coverage of unknown environments. TRACE is built upon a hierarchical coverage tree that provides a global representation of the evolving connectivity of the uncovered space. As the environment is incrementally revealed and covered, newly discovered obstacles and covered cells may fragment the remaining uncovered space into disconnected regions. TRACE recursively expands the corresponding tree nodes to explicitly represent these regions and organize them for subsequent coverage planning. Based on the updated tree, an incremental global tour is maintained to guide the coverage process. TRACE locally refines only the affected portions while preserving the visiting order of unchanged regions, thereby reducing the computational burden of global replanning and maintaining a consistent coverage progression. Guided by the global tour, a local planner generates back-and-forth coverage paths and switches to global-tour-aware planning to efficiently complete the target regions. Theoretical analysis establishes the computational complexity and complete coverage property of TRACE, and derives an approximation bound for the incremental global tour refinement. The performance of TRACE is evaluated through extensive high-fidelity simulations and real-robot experiments using a mobile robot. Comparative evaluations against six existing CPP methods demonstrate significant improvements in coverage time, path length, overlap ratio, and number of turns.

</details>


### [156] [Compact but Moving: Intervention-Relevant Geometry in Recurrent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21787)
*Yuming Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在受控循环世界模型中发现：反事实速度干预形成的紧致秩-4修正虽迅速离开原入口子空间，但其沿事实轨迹Jacobian链的切向输运仍能捕获大部分非线性修正并保留反事实未来功能，干预结构表现为随状态移动的局部几何而非固定低维状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作在学习到的循环世界模型中发现了检查点特定的秩-4界面，可一次性施加反事实速度干预；但此类修正在进入模型后究竟如何演化尚不清楚，需要判断它是否仍保持紧致结构、是否被动态封闭于某个低维状态，这关系到世界模型内部表征与可控性的基本理解。

Method: 在受控循环世界模型上开展干预分析：追踪秩-4入口方向在自主展开中偏离固定子空间的过程；用事实轨迹的循环Jacobian链输运入口方向，构造低秩图像以拟合非线性修正；在多个独立训练的结构化GRU与参数匹配的LSTM（以特权紧致修正初始化）上复现输运/功能模式；刻画全循环载体上的有限时域未来响应算子，并通过补丁式干预比较其未来敏感方向与原生反事实组织，用局部算子对注册方向面板做扰动效应排序；另做全幅值测定检查事实终点切向残差。

Result: 修正快速离开固定入口子空间，但经Jacobian链输运得到的低秩图像仍能捕获大部分非线性修正；以切向预测修正重启可保留大量反事实未来功能；该输运/功能模式在独立训练的结构化GRU与参数匹配LSTM上重复出现；未来响应算子在补丁与原生态反事实基点处能准确排序有限扰动效应，补丁使领先未来敏感方向向匹配的原生反事实组织移动；全幅值测定发现显著的事实终点切向残差，并在三个检查点中的两个支持响应重构。

Conclusion: 紧致干预结构可以作为嵌入高维循环动力学中的、随状态移动的局部几何而持续存在，而不意味着存在固定或动态封闭的低维状态。这为理解世界模型内部干预的可迁移性提供了更一般的几何视角。

Abstract: Learned world models may have compact interventions even when their recurrent state is high-dimensional, but it is unclear what happens to such a correction after it enters the model. We study this question in a controlled recurrent world model where prior work identified a checkpoint-specific rank-4 interface for one-shot counterfactual velocity interventions. The correction rapidly leaves this fixed entry subspace during autonomous rollout. Nevertheless, a low-rank image obtained by transporting the entry directions through the factual recurrent Jacobian chain continues to capture most of the nonlinear correction. Restarts using the tangent-predicted correction preserve substantial counterfactual future function. This transport/function pattern recurs across independently trained structured-GRU models and a parameter-matched LSTM initialized with a privileged compact correction. We further characterize a finite-horizon future-response operator over the full recurrent carrier. Patching shifts its leading future-sensitive directions toward the matched native-counterfactual organization, and the local operator accurately ranks finite perturbation effects over the registered direction panels at the patched and native-counterfactual basepoints. A separate full-amplitude assay finds substantial factual-endpoint tangent residuals and supports response reconfiguration in two of three checkpoints. Together, these results show that compact intervention structure can persist as a moving, state-dependent local geometry embedded in high-dimensional recurrent dynamics, without implying a fixed or dynamically closed low-dimensional state.

</details>


### [157] [From Pretraining to Proficiency: Real-World Subtask RL for Long-Horizon Manipulation with Minimal Human Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21788)
*Sichang Su,Benjamin Yang,Zhiyun Deng,Boyuan Liang,Yip Fun Yeung,Zelin Wang,Lingfeng Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PARTS：在真实机器人上针对瓶颈子任务做残差式RL微调，冻结预训练策略提供标称动作，由智能体生成的selector与成功验证器激活残差纠正并给出子任务局部奖励，结合在线RL与成功重加权再训练，双臂YAM提升32%→61%、单臂Franka提升50%→95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人基础策略在执行长时程任务时，往往整体大部分可完成却反复卡在少数关键子任务上。用全任务示教做SFT需操作者重复策略已掌握的行为，成本高；而现有RL微调仅靠稀疏奖励难以支撑长时程任务。因此需要一种把练习集中到瓶颈子任务、且尽量少人工介入的真实世界RL框架。

Method: PARTS（Policy Adaptation with RL on Targeted Subtasks）：冻结的预训练策略全程输出nominal actions；由agent生成的selector决定何时激活残差修正，成功验证器提供子任务级局部结果奖励，从而在整任务成功稀少时也能从成功的子任务学习。训练采用在线RL + success-reweighted retraining，每轮再训练的残差策略重新部署收集新经验，形成迭代循环。人在setup阶段指认瓶颈子任务，并在必要时做物理复位。

Result: 双臂YAM任务整任务成功率32%→61%，单臂Franka任务50%→95%；每任务平均仅需数十分钟真实世界RL rollout。相同机器人rollout预算下，相较现有真实世界RL微调方法，整任务成功率提升超过25%，且所需人工介入更少。

Conclusion: 将RL练习聚焦于子任务瓶颈、以冻结基础策略保底并用学习到的selector/验证器提供局部奖励，是提升长时程真实机器人任务成功率的高样本效率路径，同时显著降低人工示教与干预需求。

Abstract: A pretrained robot foundation policy may execute most of a long-horizon task yet repeatedly fail at a few critical subtasks. Collecting additional full-task demonstrations for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) requires operators to repeat behaviors the policy already performs well. Reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning offers a promising path to bridge this gap, but existing approaches struggle to solve long-horizon tasks using only sparse rewards. We present PARTS (Policy Adaptation with RL on Targeted Subtasks), a real-world subtask RL framework that concentrates practice at these bottlenecks while allowing training rollouts to proceed with minimal human intervention. The frozen pretrained policy supplies nominal actions throughout execution, while agent-generated selectors and success verifiers activate residual corrections and provide local outcome rewards. These rewards support learning from successful subtasks even when complete-task successes are scarce. Training combines online RL with success-reweighted retraining, and each retrained residual policy is redeployed to collect further experience. Humans identify bottlenecks during setup and perform physical resets when needed. On bimanual YAM and single-arm Franka tasks, PARTS improves complete-task success from 32% to 61% and from 50% to 95%, respectively, using tens of minutes of real-world RL rollouts per task on average. Compared with existing real-world RL fine-tuning methods, PARTS raises full-task success by more than 25% under the same robot-rollout budget while requiring less human involvement.

</details>


### [158] [AcousticDiffusion: Semantically Conditioned Audio-Guided Diffusion Policy for Search-and-Rescue Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21792)
*Iana Zhura,Didar Seyidov,Dmitrii Plotnikov,Hajira Amjad,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AcousticDiffusion: 语义条件化的音频引导扩散策略，用于救援机器人朝人类呼救者导航；通过语音门控、声源角色分配、麦克风阵列DOA贝叶斯BEV信念场与自运动补偿，条件化扩散模型生成路径点轨迹，在ZSL-1四足上零样本在线部署，终点距离较经典规划器降低37.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉退化或被遮挡的救援场景中，机器人需要"听声辨位"并主动接近人类呼救者。现有声源定位（如ODAS）只给出不精确的方位/距离估计，且无法区分多个说话者（呼救 vs 干扰），直接交给A*、RRT等经典规划器会导致定位误差被放大、难以真正靠近目标。因此需要一个能把不确定声学观测转化为语义化导航信念、并直接输出导航决策的策略框架。

Method: 1) 冻结的预训练音频识别器处理10.24 s窗口，加入语音门控与"求救感知"优先级排序，将识别输出转化为声源级导航角色（如HELP指定）；2) 将麦克风阵列DOA量测递归积分进机器人中心的贝叶斯鸟瞰信念场，自运动补偿对齐连续观测，逐步约束声源位置同时保留由方位引起的距离不确定性；3) 以语义信念、近期声学观测、音频特征与机器人状态为条件，训练扩散模型生成路径点轨迹（音频引导扩散策略）；4) 在ZSL-1四足上在线部署，无需额外重训练。

Result: 合成导航验证集（使用真实录音）上平均终点方位误差11.20°，91.78%轨迹落在呼救者±30°内；干扰者拒绝率89.20%–98.99%；在HELP指定说话者与竞争说话者并存时，91.07%窗口优先HELP。ZSL-1四足在线部署平均方位误差64.9°，优于A*（98.2°）与RRT（90.4°），平均规划计算时间6.07 ms；平均最终声源距离由经典规划器（基于ODAS引导）的3.96 m降至2.48 m，改善37.4%。

Conclusion: 该框架能在声学定位不完美的情况下，将不确定的声学观测转化为更贴近人类呼救者的导航行为，验证了语义条件化扩散策略在音频引导人导向导航中的有效性，并具备真实四足平台的零样本在线部署能力。

Abstract: Navigating toward human callers is an important capability for rescue robots operating where visual contact is degraded or occluded. We present AcousticDiffusion, a semantically conditioned, audio-guided diffusion policy for human-directed navigation. A frozen pretrained audio recognizer processes 10.24 s windows, with speech gating and distress-aware prioritization converting recognition outputs into source-level navigation roles. Microphone-array direction-of-arrival measurements are recursively integrated into a robot-centric Bayesian bird's-eye-view belief field. Ego-motion compensation aligns successive observations, progressively constraining source position while preserving bearing-induced range uncertainty. The semantic belief, recent acoustic observations, audio features, and robot state condition a diffusion model that generates waypoint trajectories. On a synthetic-navigation validation set using recorded audio, AcousticDiffusion achieves a mean end-point bearing error of 11.20 degrees, with 91.78% of trajectories aligned within 30 degrees of the caller. Distractor rejection ranges from 89.20% to 98.99%, and the policy favors a HELP-designated caller over a competing speaker in 91.07% of windows. Deployed online on a ZSL-1 quadruped without additional retraining, it achieves a mean bearing error of 64.9 degrees, compared with 98.2 degrees for A* and 90.4 degrees for RRT, with a mean planner compute time of 6.07 ms. Despite imperfect acoustic localization, the reported mean final source distance is reduced from 3.96 m for the classical planners using ODAS-derived (Open embedded Audition System) guidance to 2.48 m, a 37.4% improvement. These results demonstrate the framework's ability to translate uncertain acoustic observations into closer approaches to human callers.

</details>


### [159] [Contact-Rich Motion Planning via GPU-Parallel Mode Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21803)
*Jiayun Li,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoMET 将 GPU 并行轨迹评估与贪心接触模式扩展结合，重新审视接触丰富运动规划中"广域显式模式评估"的可行性，在平面推动与双臂非抓取操作任务上与优化、采样、树搜索基线相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富运动规划因接触决策组合爆炸而计算困难，现有方法多通过启发式搜索或优化重构来回避对接触模式序列的广泛评估，作者希望借助现代 GPU 硬件重新审视线搜索的可行性。

Method: 提出 CoMET：以 GPU 并行的高吞吐轨迹评估器为核心，配合贪心式接触模式扩展（Contact-Mode Expansion），在模式空间中做有原则的局部扩展而非全枚举；在平面推动基准与双臂非抓取操作任务上进行对比，并消融评估器与扩展策略的贡献。

Result: 平面推动基准上解质量与规划时间与优化类、采样类、树搜索基线相当，在几乎所有实例上匹配全枚举参考，且评估次数更少、规划时间更短；消融显示性能提升主要来自高吞吐轨迹评估器；双臂非抓取操作中，GPU 友好的局部模式扩展在模式空间增大时成功率高于所测自适应树搜索。

Conclusion: 广域显式接触模式评估结合并行轨迹优化是接触丰富运动规划的一种简单且有效的替代方案，硬件加速使曾被回避的搜索空间评估重新变得可行。

Abstract: Contact-rich motion planning (CRMP) is essential for robotic manipulation and locomotion, yet remains computationally challenging due to combinatorial contact decisions. Existing methods typically avoid broad evaluation of contact-mode sequences through search heuristics or optimization reformulations. We revisit broad evaluation in light of modern GPU hardware and introduce Contact-Mode Expansion with parallel Trajectory optimization (CoMET), which combines GPU-parallel trajectory evaluation with greedy contact-mode expansion. On planar pushing benchmarks, CoMET is competitive with optimization-based, sampling, and tree-search baselines in solution quality and planning time, matching the full-enumeration reference on nearly all instances with fewer evaluations and shorter planning times. Ablations suggest that much of the performance gain comes from the high-throughput trajectory evaluator. In bimanual nonprehensile manipulation, GPU-friendly local mode expansion achieves higher planning success than the tested adaptive tree search as the mode space grows. These results demonstrate that broad explicit mode evaluation provides a simple yet effective alternative for CRMP.

</details>


### [160] [A Sim-to-Real Integration Pipeline for Training and Deployment of Chunk-Based VLA Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21817)
*Mathilde Kappel,Clémence Grislain,Mohamed Chetouani,Olivier Sigaud,Louis Annabi,Fa\"ız Ben Amar,Stéphane Doncieux,Mahdi Khoramshahi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出开源sim-to-real实验协议：在仿真中生成专家轨迹，于真实Franka FR3上开环回放并采集视觉/本体感知观测，转为VLA训练格式；同一部署栈闭环用于策略评估，并能量化sim-to-real差距，数据集与代码已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型依赖大规模真实世界示教数据，而真机采集通常依靠遥操作，成本高、耗时长、难以规模化，成为训练与评测的瓶颈。

Method: 在仿真中生成专家轨迹，将其在真实Franka FR3上开环回放，同步记录真实视觉与本体感知观测并转换为VLA训练兼容格式；复用同一硬件部署栈进行闭环策略评估，每条真实记录与其对应仿真轨迹配对。

Result: 构建了数据采集与评估共享同一硬件配置的流程，可配对量化sim-to-real差距；开源发布所采集数据集（Hugging Face）及流水线源码。

Conclusion: 该协议以低成本、可复现的方式缓解VLA真机数据瓶颈，并提供sim-to-real差距的直接度量，具备开源可复用价值。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have become a prominent paradigm for mapping multimodal inputs, including semantic instructions, visual observations of the scene, and proprioceptive observations, to robot actions. Most state-of-the-art models predict actions in the end-effector pose space as sequences of action chunks. Training and evaluating these models requires large-scale collections of real-world demonstrations, pairing robot actions with the corresponding visual and proprioceptive observations. Collecting such data on real hardware typically relies on human teleoperation, making the process costly, time-consuming, and difficult to scale. We present an open-source sim-to-real experimental protocol that addresses this bottleneck: expert trajectories generated in simulation are replayed open-loop on a real Franka FR3 setup, where the corresponding real visual and proprioceptive observations are recorded and converted into a format compatible with VLA training. The same deployment stack is then reused, in closed-loop, to evaluate a trained policy on that setup, so that data collection and evaluation share an identical hardware configuration. Because each real recording is paired with the simulated trajectory that produced it, the protocol also yields a direct measurement of the sim-to-real gap. We release the collected datasets on Hugging Face together with the pipeline source code this https URL .

</details>


### [161] [LunaDrive: A Delay-Compensated High-Voltage GaN FET-Based Motor Driver for Dynamic Robots with Flat BLDC Motors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21818)
*Sota Yuzaki,Temma Suzuki,Hiromi Tada,Masanori Konishi,Kento Kawaharazuka,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了基于GaN FET的高功率扁平BLDC电机驱动器，支持96 V/30 A连续、80 A峰值、3110 Hz电频率，并研究高速区延迟补偿，24S LiPo电池负载举升实验验证了动态机器人适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商用伺服电机多限于48 V以下，限制最高转速，而动态机器人需要瞬时高能量输出；Si MOSFET驱动器难以兼顾高耐压与大电流，GaN FET虽具潜力但可贴装于扁平BLDC电机后侧的紧凑驱动器仍很有限。

Method: 采用GaN FET设计面向高功率扁平BLDC电机的紧凑型电机驱动器，并针对超出额定工作范围的高速区进行延迟补偿研究；通过加装散热片的96 V工况实验与24S LiPo电池（100 V）驱动的高速负载举升实验进行验证。

Result: 96 V下实现30 A连续电流（带散热片）、80 A峰值电流和3110 Hz最高电频率；24S LiPo电池驱动的负载举升实验证明其可用于动态机器人场景。

Conclusion: GaN FET驱动器可在超额定电压下提升瞬时最高转速，配合高速延迟补偿，满足动态机器人的高功率伺服需求。

Abstract: The performance improvement of high-power flat BLDC motors has accelerated the development of dynamic robots. However, many commercially available servo motors assume operating voltages of 48 V or lower, which limits the maximum rotational speed. Dynamic robots require rapid energy generation, so this voltage constraint restricts motion performance. Therefore, driving motors beyond the rated voltage is desirable to increase the instantaneous maximum speed. On the other hand, semiconductor devices used in motor drivers have a trade-off between voltage rating and current capacity. Conventional drivers using Si MOSFETs have difficulty achieving both high-voltage and high-current operation. Although GaN FETs are promising, compact drivers that can be mounted on the rear side of flat BLDC motors remain limited. In this study, a motor driver for high-power flat BLDC motors using GaN FETs is developed. The effect of delay compensation in the high-speed region beyond the rated operating range is also investigated. In the experiments, under 96 V operation, a continuous current of 30 A was achieved with a heat sink attached. A peak current of 80 A and a maximum electrical frequency of 3110 Hz were confirmed. A high-speed load lifting experiment driven by a LiPo battery 24S (100 V) was also conducted, demonstrating applicability to dynamic robot operation.

</details>


### [162] [PopNavShift: Stress-Testing Social Navigation under Behavioral Population Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21838)
*Kaizhen Tan,Diyu Zheng,Tim Guangyu Wu,ChengHe Guan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PopNavShift 仿真框架，用 LLM 生成的合成人格记录构造"人群条件化"的行人运动参数，在 7488 次匹配实验中对三种社交导航策略做分布鲁棒性压力测试，发现行人负担指标（平均延迟、最差十分位延迟）对人群分布变化远比机器人自身行驶时间敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交导航算法通常在固定行人行为分布下评测，但真实中行人对机器人的反应因个体与社会情境差异很大。缺乏在"行人群体分布偏移"下对策略排序稳定性的匹配评测手段，导致结论可能过拟合单一行为分布。

Method: 构建 PopNavShift 匹配仿真框架：用 600 条合成 persona 记录（来自 MatrAIx Persona 1M）驱动 LLM 生成响应，并确定性映射为有界运动参数（如速度、让行倾向、时间压力），得到人群条件化的行人运动剖面；在 8 种人群条件、三种代表性策略（反应式避让、提前让行、互惠避碰）下进行 7488 次匹配机器人运行；另做同一 202 个 persona 的匹配干预实验（仅改时间压力），并用第二种行人动力学模型做稳健性验证。

Result: 仅改变时间压力时，8.6% 的控制器排序在机器人行驶时间上反转，而在平均行人延迟上反转 22.4%、最差十分位延迟上反转 23.9%。跨人群条件下，这种敏感性对行人负担大于对机器人行驶时间，且在空间受限场景中加剧；换用第二套行人动力学模型后定性模式保持一致。

Conclusion: 社交导航策略的评测应当跨行为人群进行，并同时报告机器人性能与行人负担两类指标，否则策略排序与结论可能不稳健。PopNavShift 提供了可复用的匹配压力测试范式。

Abstract: Social-navigation algorithms are often evaluated under a fixed pedestrian-behavior distribution, despite substantial variation in pedestrian responses to robots across individuals and social contexts. We introduce PopNavShift, a matched simulation framework for stress-testing social-navigation strategies under pedestrian population shifts. PopNavShift constructs population-conditioned pedestrian motion profiles by prompting Gemini 3.7 Flash with 600 synthetic persona records from MatrAIx Persona 1M and deterministically mapping the responses into bounded motion parameters. It then compares three representative navigation strategies, reactive avoidance, early yielding, and reciprocal collision avoidance, across eight population conditions and 7,488 matched robot runs. In a matched intervention on the same 202 personas, changing only time pressure reverses 8.6% of controller rankings based on robot travel time, but 22.4% based on mean pedestrian delay and 23.9% based on worst-decile delay. Across population conditions, this sensitivity is greater for pedestrian burden than for robot travel time and increases in spatially constrained settings; the same qualitative pattern persists under a second pedestrian dynamics model. These findings support evaluating navigation strategies across behavioral populations using both robot performance and pedestrian burden.

</details>


### [163] [VIRGA: Virtual-Agent-Intermediated Riemannian Geometry for Active-Sensing Air-Ground Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21883)
*Fenghe Guo,Runjie Shen,Chenyang Sun,Junrui Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VIRGA，一种神经几何协调框架：将空地双LiDAR观测映射为有界、源特定的黎曼场，并通过带互惠弹性反馈的虚拟智能体耦合，实现UAV-云台-UGV闭环协同观测与避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空地自主中的难点在于：UAV必须持续处于UGV云台LiDAR的可观测范围内，同时还要规避动态障碍，并协调异构运动、有限感知与任务主导权的切换。现有控制器（Ray-RMP、解析黎曼场、ColAG）在安全间隙、视场维持或延迟上各有明显短板。

Method: VIRGA将双LiDAR观测转成有界的源特定黎曼场（Riemannian fields），通过虚拟智能体与互惠弹性反馈耦合两平台；再由平台感知执行映射把共享协调参考转化为可行的UAV、UGV与云台指令，并施加主动观测安全约束；含在线几何评估、虚拟智能体中介、互惠反馈三个消融模块。

Result: 对比三个基线：Ray-RMP响应最快但耦合任务下安全间隙不足；稠密解析黎曼场几何避障更好但计算代价高、无法稳定维持视场；ColAG延迟最低但仍有安全与可观测性违规。VIRGA在仓库成对工况全部安全完成，且在未重训练的洞穴长距压力测试中于不规则狭窄几何下保持持续协调。

Conclusion: 虚拟智能体中介的弹性耦合与在线黎曼几何评估，是空地持续可观测协同的关键；框架具备跨场景泛化能力（无需重训练）。

Abstract: Air-ground autonomy becomes harder when the unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) must remain observable by a gimbal light detection and ranging (LiDAR) mounted on the unmanned ground vehicle (UGV). The platforms must avoid dynamic obstacles while coordinating heterogeneous motion, limited sensing, and changing task initiative within one closed loop. This paper presents VIRGA, a neural geometric coordination framework that turns dual-LiDAR observations into bounded source-specific Riemannian fields and couples them through a virtual agent with reciprocal elastic feedback. Platform-aware execution maps convert the shared coordination reference into feasible UAV, UGV, and gimbal commands while enforcing active-observation safeguards. Evaluation against three complementary baselines reveals distinct limitations. An adapted Ray-RMP controller provides the fastest Riemannian response but produces insufficient clearance in the coupled air-ground task. A dense analytical Riemannian field improves geometric avoidance, yet its high evaluation cost prevents stable field-of-view maintenance. An adapted ColAG controller achieves the lowest latency but still incurs safety and observability violations. VIRGA completes all paired warehouse conditions safely, while a long-range cave stress test without retraining demonstrates sustained coordination in irregular and confined geometry. Ablations confirm contributions from online geometric evaluation, virtual-agent mediation, and reciprocal feedback.

</details>


### [164] [CommitFlow: Semantic Commitment Verification and Local Correction for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation VLA Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21908)
*Zixiang Zhao,Yansong Feng,Yang Yang,Chaoyu Wang,Haoran Xiao,Hui Zhang,Chuang Cheng,Jianjun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CommitFlow 提出闭环执行框架，通过语义承诺监控（SCM）、局部纠正生成（BoundaryFlow）与关系/增益校准（RGC）在不微调基座策略的前提下修正长时程执行中的语义-物理状态错配，在 RoboTwin 2.0 十项任务上平均成功率 75.9%，较 pi0.5 提升 22.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略在长时程执行中，常在前一阶段的物理效果尚未真正建立时就推进到下一语义阶段，即"语义承诺"与实际物理状态之间存在错配。仅靠动作指令无法确认物理条件是否成立，局部偏差会沿任务链传播并导致失败，因此需要显式的条件监控与局部修正机制。

Method: CommitFlow 保持基座策略冻结，构建闭环执行：1）Semantic Commitment Monitor（SCM）将阶段要求与当前状态证据比对，在关键条件未满足或被破坏时暂缓后续依赖动作；2）BoundaryFlow 以当前状态与基座动作为条件生成局部纠正动作；3）Relation and Gain Calibration（RGC）在满足约束的前提下选择最小纠正强度，避免过度干预。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 十项常见任务上平均成功率达 75.9%，相较基座策略 pi0.5 提升 22.7%；跨策略实验显示一致的增益，表明该方法具备策略无关性。

Conclusion: 通过将语义阶段承诺与物理状态证据显式对齐、并以最小强度做局部纠正，可在不重训基座策略的情况下显著改善长时程机器人执行的可靠性。

Abstract: Although vision-language-action (VLA) policies have advanced rapidly, long-horizon execution may still progress to the next task stage before the required physical effect has been established. We call this a mismatch between semantic commitments, physical conditions that a stage must establish or maintain, and the actual physical state. Because an action command alone cannot confirm such a condition, local deviations can propagate and cause task failure. To address this problem, we present CommitFlow, a closed-loop execution framework that combines commitment monitoring with local correction while keeping the base policy frozen. CommitFlow integrates three components. A Semantic Commitment Monitor (SCM) compares stage requirements against current state evidence and holds back dependent actions when a required condition is unmet or violated. BoundaryFlow then generates a local correction conditioned on the current state and base action, and Relation and Gain Calibration (RGC) selects the smallest correction strength that satisfies the relevant constraints. Across the ten common RoboTwin 2.0 benchmark tasks, CommitFlow achieves a mean success rate of 75.9 percent, improving on the base policy pi0.5 by 22.7 percent. Cross-policy experiments show consistent gains, pointing toward reliable long-horizon robot execution.

</details>


### [165] [MAAP: Multi-Agent Active Perception for Collaborative Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21929)
*Bruno N.Y. Chen,Li Kang,Heng Zhou,Xiufeng Song,Zhemeng Zhang,Jiahua Ma,Yiran Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MAAP将每个机械臂的腕部相机视为团队共享的移动视角，实现"操作即主动感知"；RAIL控制器预测每条臂的当前角色并以其为条件生成动作。仿真中平均成功率从固定视角56.5%提升至全腕视角70.0%，MAAP+RAIL达79.2%；真机双臂20次放置试验成功14次（固定视角ACT为0次）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多臂操作中每条臂自带腕部相机，天然产生多个任务驱动的视角，但这些观测通常被浪费；主动感知在操作中往往被当作需要专门传感智能体的独立任务。作者希望在不额外增加感知机械臂的前提下，利用操作臂自身的运动与相机作为主动感知机制。

Method: 提出MAAP（Multi-Agent Active Perception）：每条臂双用途，既执行操作动作又作为团队的移动视角；配套RAIL（Role-Aware Imitation Learning）控制器，预测每条臂当前角色与动作块，并将角色作为条件用于动作生成，在同一网络中表达角色依赖的动作。在4个仿真任务与一个双臂真机平台上评测。

Result: 仿真：固定相机56.5%、单一主动腕视角62.5%、全部腕视角70.0%、MAAP+RAIL 79.2%。RAIL的增益集中在三臂Microwave任务，相同多腕输入下从47%升至82%。真机双臂平台上，MAAP+RAIL在20次放置试验中成功14次，而固定视角ACT为0/20。

Conclusion: 协同操作本身即可作为一种主动感知机制：无需专用传感智能体，让操作臂的腕部相机与角色感知策略耦合，就能显著提升多臂操作成功率，并在真机上验证了固定视角方法完全失效时的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-agent manipulation naturally produces multiple task-driven viewpoints: every arm carries a wrist camera and moves through the scene while acting. Yet these observations are typically underutilized, and active perception in manipulation is still often treated as requiring a dedicated sensing agent. We introduce MAAP (Multi-Agent Active Perception), in which every arm is dual-purpose: it executes manipulation actions and, through the wrist camera it carries, simultaneously serves as a moving viewpoint for the team. We pair this with RAIL (Role-Aware Imitation Learning), a controller that predicts each arm's current role alongside its action chunk and conditions action generation on it, representing role-dependent actions within one network. Across four simulated tasks, widening the perception regime lifts average success from 56.5% with a fixed camera to 62.5% with one active wrist view and 70.0% with all of them, while MAAP+RAIL reaches 79.2%. RAIL's additional gain is concentrated on the three-arm Microwave task, where success rises from 47% to 82% on identical multi-wrist inputs. On a dual-arm platform, MAAP+RAIL succeeds in 14 of 20 placement trials compared with 0 of 20 for fixed-view ACT. Collaborative manipulation can thus serve as an active perception mechanism in its own right.

</details>


### [166] [When Should a Failing Robot Ask? Initiating Corrective Human-Robot Dialogue from Audited Sensor Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21942)
*Eshika Pathak,Leela Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文构建了失败原因已知的机器人纠错对话仿真基准，测量相机与力传感器各自揭示的信息量（力数据诊断准确率0.99，图像方法最高0.55），并测试六个开源视觉语言模型做出的“自行诊断/调用其他传感器/询问人类”三动作决策。结果发现模型行为受提示表面形式（如拒答选项位置）左右，其准确率不超过多数类基线且置信度无信息量；将力数据以文本形式提供后有四个模型首次超过基线，说明失败主要源于缺少传感器数据而非能力不足；模型不遵循由实测准确率导出的最优决策策略，询问率也不随问题成本四倍变化而改变，但一次人类询问可将其准确率提升至接近回答者自身可靠度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人失败后如何选择纠错行动（自行诊断、调用其他机载传感器或询问人类）取决于传感器信息量与自身诊断可靠性，但现有视觉语言模型是否具备这种基于证据的决策能力缺乏可控评估，需要一个真实失败原因已知、可量化各传感器信息贡献的基准来检验。

Method: 构建失败原因被注入而因此真值已知的机器人纠错对话仿真基准，在显式防止数据泄漏的前提下量化各传感器对失败原因的诊断能力；随后测试六个开源视觉语言模型在多种提示变体（含拒答选项位置变化、是否给出示例）下的诊断与询问行为，并将该选择形式化为“自行行动/咨询自身传感器/询问人类”的三动作决策问题，用实测准确率与成本推导最优策略并与模型行为对比。

Result: 部分失败可由相机图像诊断，另一些仅能由力数据诊断（力数据达0.99，图像方法均不超过0.55）；拒答选项从末位移到首位使三个模型及家族组合的拒答率从78-100%骤降至0-6%；所有提示变体下基于帧的准确率均不高于多数类基线，显式置信度与正确性无关；将力数据以十行文本提供后，六个模型中有四个首次超过基线；模型不遵循导出的最优策略，询问率对问题成本四倍变化不敏感，但当其询问人类时准确率可达0.70-0.81。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型的纠错决策行为由提示表面形式而非证据驱动，其失败在很大程度上源于缺失传感器数据而非能力缺陷；是否询问人类应依据实测准确率与显式成本来决定，而不应依赖模型自报置信度。

Abstract: A robot that fails at a task faces the first decision in corrective dialogue: act on its own diagnosis, consult another onboard sensor, or interrupt a person. Choosing well requires knowing how much the robot's sensors reveal about the cause and how reliable the robot's own diagnosis is. We build a simulated benchmark in which every failure's true cause is known, because we injected it, and measure what each sensor reveals, with explicit checks against data leakage. Some failures are diagnosable from camera images; others only from the robot's force data (0.99 from force data, no image method above 0.55). We then test six open vision-language models. Their behavior tracks the surface of the prompt, not the evidence: moving the refusal option from last to first in the answer list collapses refusal rates from 78-100% to 0-6% in three of the six swept model-and-family pairs. Accuracy from frames stays at or below a majority-class baseline under every prompt variant, with or without worked examples, and stated confidence carries no information about correctness. Handing the same models the force data as ten lines of text produces the first above-baseline diagnoses, in four of the six models: much of the failure reflects missing sensor data, not missing ability. We pose the choice as a three-action decision problem, act, consult your own sensors, or ask a human, whose optimal policy follows from measured accuracy. The models do not follow it, and their ask rates ignore a fourfold change in question cost. One question to a human still lifts them from that baseline to roughly the answerer's own reliability (0.70-0.81 when they ask). The decision to ask should be tied to measured accuracy and stated costs, not to the model's confidence.

</details>


### [167] [GALA: Geometry-Aware Latent Action Modeling for Vision-Language-Action Model Pretraining across Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21948)
*Yichen Liu,Puzhen Yuan,Xiang Zhu,Yanjiang Guo,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GALA 提出几何感知的潜在动作建模框架，用统一末端执行器运动表示（UEMR）将 3D 末端几何运动注入图像潜在动作，实现跨本体（含无动作自我中心人类视频）的细粒度动作表征预训练，用于 VLA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像式潜在动作模型（LAM）从多本体数据中学到本体无关的动作表征，但难以捕捉末端执行器的细粒度关节运动，尤其是人手与灵巧手的指级几何变化；而直接引入点云会带来细粒度强但共享语义弱的问题，妨碍跨本体预训练。

Method: 提出 GALA：以 UEMR（统一末端执行器运动表示）在保留细粒度运动信息的同时提升跨本体泛化性；将捕捉场景级动态的视觉潜在动作与捕捉共享细粒度末端关节运动的几何潜在动作相结合，为多本体数据（含无动作自我中心人类视频）上的 VLA 预训练提供监督信号。

Result: 细粒度运动探测、跨本体检索与下游 VLA 评估均验证有效性；RoboCasa-GR1 成功率 68.3%，真实世界成功率 75.5%。

Conclusion: 几何感知的统一末端运动表示是提升跨本体细粒度动作表征与 VLA 预训练的有效途径，且能利用无动作视频。

Abstract: Learning large-scale vision-language-action (VLA) models from multi-embodiment datasets remains challenging due to heterogeneous action spaces across end effectors. Although latent action models (LAMs) can learn embodiment-agnostic action representations from diverse video data, existing image-based LAMs often fail to capture fine-grained end-effector articulation, particularly finger-level geometric changes in human and dexterous robot hands. To address this limitation, we propose GALA, a Geometry-Aware Latent-Action modeling framework that augments image-based latent actions with 3D end-effector geometric motion. However, naively incorporating point clouds yields fine-grained action representations with limited shared semantics, hindering cross-embodiment pretraining. To address this issue, we introduce the Unified End-effector Motion Representation (UEMR), which preserves fine-grained motion information while improving the cross-embodiment generalizability of latent actions. Building upon UEMR, GALA combines visual latent actions that capture scene-level dynamics with geometric latent actions that capture shared fine-grained end-effector articulation, providing effective supervision for VLA pretraining from multi-embodiment data, including action-free ego-centric human videos. Experiments on fine-grained motion probing, cross-embodiment retrieval, and downstream VLA evaluation demonstrate GALA's effectiveness in modeling generalizable fine-grained motions across embodiments, achieving 68.3% RoboCasa-GR1 success rate and 75.5% real-world success rate. Code, appendix, and demos are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [168] [CARF: Contrastive Attraction-Repulsion of Failure-Guided Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21982)
*Shuqi Zhao,Bang Du,Cheng-En Wu,Yichen Xie,Yixiao Wang,Masayoshi Tomizuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CARF框架通过对比式"吸引-排斥"学习从含失败的不完美机器人数据中学习：用仅在成功示范及其扰动上训练的进度评分器识别失败轨迹中的进展段与致失败段，以统一流匹配目标吸引进展行为、排斥致失败行为并排除歧义段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有利用失败轨迹的方法多只挖掘"仍有进展"的片段，忽视了直接导致任务失败的"致失败行为"。作者认为这两类片段提供本质不对称的监督信号：进展段应被模仿，致失败段应被显式回避。

Method: 提出CARF（Contrastive Attraction-Repulsion of Failure-guided）框架。1）进度重要性评分器仅用成功专家示范及其扰动训练，估计每步对任务完成的贡献，从而在失败轨迹中定位信息性区域；2）用这些分数驱动统一的流匹配（flow-matching）目标：对进展行为施加吸引项、对致失败行为施加排斥项，并排除歧义片段；3）整体为对比式吸引-排斥策略学习，避免来自歧义失败段的不可靠监督。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界的多种失败场景中，CARF相对于竞争基线取得一致性提升；消融实验进一步验证了所提评分机制与吸引-排斥机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 将失败轨迹中的进展段与致失败段视作不对称监督（模仿 vs 回避），能更充分利用不完美数据，并规避歧义失败段的不可靠监督。

Abstract: Robot demonstration collection often produces imperfect or failed trajectories in addition to successful demonstrations. Existing methods typically exploit failed trajectories by identifying segments that still make progress toward task completion, but largely overlook \textit{failure-critical behaviors} that directly lead to task failure. Here we argue that these two types of segments provide fundamentally asymmetric supervision: progressive segments should be imitated, whereas failure-critical segments should be explicitly avoided. Based on this observation, we propose CARF, a Contrastive Attraction-Repulsion of Failure-guided framework for learning from imperfect robot data. CARF introduces a progress-based importance scorer, trained solely on successful expert demonstrations and its perturbation results, to estimate step-wise contributions toward task completion and identify informative regions in failed trajectories. These scores guide a unified flow-matching objective that attracts the policy toward progressive behaviors and repels it from failure-critical ones, while excluding ambiguous segments. This enables more comprehensive utilization of imperfect data and avoids unreliable supervision from ambiguous failure segments. Extensive experiments in simulation and the real world demonstrate consistent improvements over competing baselines across diverse failure scenarios, with ablations further validating the effectiveness of the proposed scoring and attraction-repulsion mechanisms. Our website is this https URL .

</details>


### [169] [SkelWAM: A Skeleton-Guided World-Action Model for Zero-Shot Cross-Embodiment Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21983)
*Pengjun Niu,Yujia Xie,Rui Peng,Hang Zhao,Ke Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SkelWAM 用统一的骨架几何世界-动作模型实现单源跨本体操作迁移，在 LIBERO-Cross10 上以 43.3% 成功率大幅超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨本体复用操作经验对扩展机器人学习、减少重复数据采集很重要，但本体变化会改变视觉外观、动作维度与语义，以及实现同一工具位姿的全身构型。作者希望不依赖目标任务的示教与策略更新，实现单源跨本体迁移。

Method: 提出骨架引导的世界-动作模型 SkelWAM：以手臂中心线几何、TCP 位姿、平行夹爪指令构成共享 25 维状态，统一规范化的第三人称与腕部观测以及未来全身动作目标；用预测式视觉监督训练视频-动作混合 Transformer 预测规范化骨架动作块，再由本体特定约束解码器转换为关节或连续体机器人控制。无需一一关节对应。

Result: 提出 LIBERO-Cross10 源域跨本体迁移基准（10任务、10个目标本体、4类形态）。Franka 训练的策略在 1000 回合中达 43.3% 成功率，超最佳基线 36.2 个百分点；并将 JAKA mini2 训练的策略部署到 Feagine A03 连续体机器人完成三项桌面操作。

Conclusion: 显式几何共享表示为跨本体感知-控制耦合提供了有效途径，可免目标示教与策略更新地迁移到异构本体，并具备真实机器人部署潜力。

Abstract: Reusing manipulation experience across robot embodiments is important for scaling robot learning and reducing repeated task-specific data collection. However, changes in embodiment alter visual appearance, action dimensionality and semantics, and the whole-body configurations that can realize the same tool pose. We present SkelWAM, a skeleton-guided world-action model that couples perception and control through one explicit geometric representation for single-source cross-embodiment manipulation. Arm centerline geometry, tool-center-point (TCP) pose, and parallel-jaw commands form a shared 25-D state. The same definition underlies canonical third-person and wrist observations and future whole-body action targets. Trained with predictive visual supervision, a video-action mixture of transformers predicts canonical skeleton action chunks, which embodiment-specific constrained decoders convert into joint or continuum-robot controls. This formulation requires no one-to-one joint correspondence and uses no target-task demonstrations or target policy updates. We introduce LIBERO-Cross10, a source-only cross-embodiment transfer benchmark covering ten tasks and ten target embodiments across four morphological groups. On this benchmark, Franka-trained SkelWAM achieves 43.3% success over 1,000 episodes, exceeding the best-performing evaluated baseline by 36.2 percentage points. We further deploy a JAKA mini2-trained policy on the Feagine A03 continuum robot for three tabletop manipulation tasks, illustrating the approach's potential for real-world cross-embodiment manipulation. Project page: this http URL

</details>


### [170] [Gripper-Aware Automatic Dense Packing of Irregular Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22062)
*Tianhao Qin,Connor McCann,Berk Calli,Jing Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出闭环密集堆叠流水线：感知＋含夹爪的放置优化＋力控执行，在Franka实机上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓储自动密集堆叠是机器人操作的难题；既有不规则物体堆叠工作多限于理想接触的仿真，把物体当孤立刚体，夹爪仅作事后离散可行性检查，且忽略执行中的感知与接触漂移。

Method: 将物体与夹爪建模为分层球树复合体；在GPU上以CMA-ES搜索5自由度位姿，垂直坐标依据当前高度图解析确定；执行时力监测竖直下压、首次接触即停；放后加一次固结推挤消除夹爪感知规划残留的横向间隙；每次放置后重新感知容器以抑制漂移累积。

Result: 在Franka Emika Panda上对3D打印平面/曲面/凹面物体集与YCB子集完成堆叠；消融实验分离了夹爪感知优化、固结推挤与网格几何对端到端成功率、达成密度与计算代价的贡献；与高度图最小化基线方法对比。

Conclusion: 夹爪感知规划＋力控执行＋重感知闭环可显著提升真实机器人不规则物体密集堆叠的可行性与密度。

Abstract: Automatic dense packing is widely desired in warehouse operations but remains a fundamental challenge in robotic manipulation. Existing work on irregular-object packing largely targets simulation with idealized contact, treating the object as an isolated rigid body. The gripper often enters as a discrete, post-hoc feasibility check, if considered at all, and the perception and contact drift accumulated during execution are not addressed. We present a closed-loop pipeline that integrates perception, gripper-aware placement optimization, and force-guided execution on a real manipulator. The optimizer represents the object together with the gripper as a single composite body of hierarchical sphere trees. It searches over five degrees of freedom on a GPU within a CMA-ES framework, with the vertical coordinate grounded analytically against the current heightmap. During execution, a force-monitored vertical descent stops on first contact. A post-release consolidation push then closes the residual lateral clearance that gripper-aware planning leaves behind. The container is re-perceived between placements so that drift does not accumulate. We validate the system on a Franka Emika Panda robot packing a 3D-printed set of flat, curved, and concave objects, and a YCB object subset. An ablation study isolates the contribution of gripper-aware optimization, the consolidation push, and mesh-derived geometry to end-to-end success, achieved density, and computational cost. We further benchmark against the heightmap-minimization method as a baseline representative of prior irregular-object packing work.

</details>


### [171] [Duty Factor Predicts Robust Constrained Quadrupedal Locomotion Across Gait Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22073)
*James Zhu,David Ologan,George Ortiz,Thomas Chun Fai Lee,Selvin Garcia Gonzalez,Ardalan Tajbakhsh,Pinhas Ben-Tzvi,Aaron M. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究表明，在四足机器人运动中，占空比（duty factor）比名义步态类型更能预测局部误差收敛，并可作为适应狭窄地形下运动鲁棒性的低维调节参数，在轨迹优化、学习控制器与质心MPC三种架构下均成立，并通过实物实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 步态类型（走/小跑）常被用来刻画四足运动，但同一步态类型下占空比、速度、站宽等参数可大幅变化，因此步态类型不足以唯一刻画运动特性，需要找到更本质的参数来描述与调节运动鲁棒性。

Method: 采用三种不同的四足运动控制方法进行研究：1）全身轨迹优化配LQR反馈；2）学习型运动控制器用于占空比选择；3）质心模型预测控制（centroidal MPC）框架，并在真实四足机器人上进行狭窄地形实验验证。

Result: 占空比比名义步态类型是更强的局部误差收敛预测因子；占空比可作为低维参数用于在狭窄地形中调节运动鲁棒性；这些趋势在质心MPC框架下依然成立，并获得实物四足狭窄地形实验的验证。

Conclusion: 占空比为跨步态类型和跨控制架构理解与选取鲁棒四足运动提供了简单而有效的基础参数。

Abstract: Quadrupedal robots are increasingly deployed in environments where locomotion must remain robust to disturbances and constrained terrain. Gait type, such as walking or trotting, is commonly used to characterize quadrupedal locomotion. However, gait type does not uniquely define locomotion, as parameters such as duty factor, speed, and stance width can vary within a single gait type. In this work, we investigate the relationship between these gait parameters using three distinct quadrupedal locomotion control approaches. First, using whole body trajectory optimization with LQR feedback, we show that duty factor is a stronger predictor of local error convergence than nominal gait type. Second, we investigate duty factor selection with a learned locomotion controller, suggesting how duty factor may serve as a low-dimensional parameter for adapting locomotion robustness in narrow-terrain environments. Finally, we show that these trends persist under a centroidal model predictive control framework and validate them through narrow-terrain experiments on a physical quadruped. These results show that duty factor provides a simple and effective basis for understanding and selecting robust quadrupedal locomotion across gait types and control architectures.

</details>


### [172] [LIMBO: Learning and Internalizing Model-Free Barrier Objectives for Agile and Safe Whole-Body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22075)
*Jake Gonzales,Arturo Flores Alvarez,Yu-Ming Chen,Aaron D. Ames,Lillian J. Ratliff,Manikantan Nambi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LIMBO 从黑盒转移数据中学习状态-动作 Q-CBF，将其安全结构蒸馏进任务策略，在 29 自由度人形机器人上实现躲避球规避与低障碍下行走，无需在线安全滤波即可迁移到真机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身安全控制需在高维非线性动力学下同时兼顾避碰与平衡，安全证书的设计与跨行为复用困难；现有 CBF 方法难以扩展到全身控制维度，且部署时通常依赖在线安全滤波器。

Method: 在冻结的基座控制器周围的残差动作空间上，用黑盒转移与基于状态的失败规范学习状态-动作控制屏障函数（Q-CBF），使合成在完整控制维度上可行且证书位于任务策略的控制空间；合成阶段用学得的安全值做风险引导采样以逼近可恢复性边界；任务学习阶段把该安全值作为教师提供动作级安全反馈，蒸馏为鲁棒任务策略。

Result: 在 29 自由度人形机器人上验证躲避球规避与低障碍下行走；同等安全规范下仅改变采样集中度，可产生从蹲伏到新颖后仰 limbo 动作的不同策略；所学策略无在线安全滤波直接迁移到硬件。

Conclusion: 学习型安全综合可扩展至敏捷全身控制；风险引导边界采样为探索可恢复性边界提供了有理论依据的途径。

Abstract: Safe whole-body control requires coordinating collision avoidance and balance under high-dimensional, nonlinear dynamics--making safety certificates difficult to design and reuse across behaviors. We present LIMBO, a framework for synthesizing a state-action control barrier function and distilling its safety structure into a task policy. LIMBO learns the safety certificate from black-box transitions and a state-based failure specification over residual actions around a frozen base controller, making Q-CBF synthesis tractable in the full control dimension while placing the certificate in the task policy's control space. During synthesis, the learned safety value drives risk-guided sampling near the estimated boundary of recoverability; during task learning, it serves as a teacher that provides action-level safety feedback, yielding a robust task policy and alleviating the need for an online safety filter at deployment. We demonstrate LIMBO on a 29-degree-of-freedom humanoid performing dodgeball avoidance and locomotion beneath low obstacles. Beyond scaling learned Q-CBFs to whole-body control, we show that risk-guided boundary sampling provides a theoretically grounded way to explore the edge of recoverability. Under the same safety specification, ceteris paribus, varying the sampling concentration produces strategies ranging from crouching to a novel backward-leaning limbo maneuver. In both settings, the learned policies transfer to hardware without online safety filtering, showing that learned safety synthesis scales to agile whole-body control.

</details>


### [173] [SeeQ: Training Generalist Value Functions for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22085)
*Saksham Singh,Zheyuan Hu,Max Sobol Mark,Jeffrey Yu,Zackory Erickson,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SeeQ 通过学习"当前活跃子任务"层级的 Q 函数来缓解长时程机器人任务中信用分配困难的问题，并用自回归自然语言预测活跃子任务替代人工标注与模块化预测系统，在真实双臂平台上提升了 best-of-N 策略引导效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略在包含多阶段、需反复尝试与 deliberation 的长时程任务上仍然脆弱。Q 值函数可通过对候选动作排序或引导策略改进来提升性能，但基于任务级稀疏奖励学习面临信用分配时域过长、Bellman 备份困难、数据覆盖要求高等问题。

Method: 训练阶段利用离线机器人数据中已有的子任务级标注提供任务分解，将 Q 值学习缩短到"当前活跃子任务"的短时域，从而用时序差分（TD）目标有效学习，并可从广泛、次优的数据中学习。测试阶段为摆脱人工标注或模块化子任务预测器，Q 函数架构先以自然语言自回归预测活跃子任务、再估计其价值。以视觉-语言骨干实现 SeeQ，在多样开源机器人操作数据上预训练，再在下游任务微调。

Result: 在两个双臂机器人平台的四个真实世界操作任务上，SeeQ 价值函数显著提升了 best-of-N 策略引导的效果。

Conclusion: 子任务层级的 Q 函数是缓解长时程信用分配难题的有效途径，且通过自回归语言式子任务预测可在测试时免去人工标注与外部子任务预测模块，为通用机器人策略提供可对齐的评估与改进信号。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress, generalist robot policies remain brittle on complex, long-horizon tasks that comprise multiple stages or require repeated attempts and deliberation on the same underlying stage before success. Q-value functions can improve these policies by ranking candidate actions or guiding policy improvement, but learning from sparse task-level rewards entails long credit-assignment horizons, difficult Bellman backups, and broad data-coverage requirements. We introduce SeeQ (Subtask-elicited Q-functions), which instead learns Q-values for the currently active subtask. This shortens the value-prediction horizon and enables effective learning with temporal-difference (TD) objectives. During training, subtask-level annotations present in offline robot data provide the decomposition and enable learning from broad, potentially suboptimal robot datasets. To eliminate the need for human annotations or modular subtask prediction systems at test time, our Q-function architecture is trained to autoregressively predict the active subtask in natural language before estimating its value. We instantiate SeeQ using a base vision-language backbone, pretrain it on diverse open-source robot manipulation data, and finetune it on downstream tasks. Across four real-world manipulation tasks on two bimanual robot platforms, the SeeQ value function substantially improves best-of-N policy steering.

</details>
